# The One After The Superbowl, Part 1
> *超级杯之后的那一集，第一部分*

The One After the Superbowl
> 超级杯之后的那个

Originally written by Michael Borkow, Mike Sikowitz & Jeffrey Astrof
> 最初由Michael Borkow、Mike Sikowitz和Jeffrey Astrof撰写

Transcribed by Joshua Hodge.
> 由约书亚·霍奇转录。

Minor additions and adjustments by Dan Silverstein.
> Dan Silverstein的轻微添加和调整。

---

🎬 *In a TV commercial that the gang is watching at Monica and Rachel's.*
> 🎬 *在莫妮卡和瑞秋家看的电视广告中*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：使役动词 make / 定语从句 / 状语从句 / 名词从句

[A guy is sitting at his desk and his boss comes in and drops a huge pile of papers on his desk. The guy looks dejected.]
> [一个家伙坐在他的办公桌旁，他的老板进来，把一大堆文件扔在他的办公桌上。这个家伙看起来很沮丧。]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="0.0" duration="3.288" /> **COMMERCIAL VOICEOVER:** <Note>Can't get the monkey off your back</Note>? Then put it in your mouth...
> 不能让猴子从你背上下来吗？然后把它放进你的嘴里。。。
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,习语">

- **Can't (you) get the monkey off your back?**：否定疑问句口语中常省略主语 you——"难道摆脱不掉吗？"；**get the monkey off your back** 是习语"摆脱心头的负担/烦恼"，广告双关：既指真猴子，也指烦恼（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md) 疑问句）
- **put it in your mouth**：祈使句承接上文，it 指代 monkey（啤酒 mascot）（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md) 祈使语气）

</GrammarTip>

[A monkey jumps on the desk and hands the guy a beer. He opens the beer and is suddenly on the beach, in a hammock, with beautiful women all around.]
> [一只猴子跳到桌子上，递给那家伙一杯啤酒。他打开啤酒，突然躺在海滩上的吊床上，周围都是美女。]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="3.288" duration="6.192" /> **COMMERCIAL VOICEOVER:** ...With MonkeyShine Beer. [MonkeyShine theme] MonkeyShine Beer, <Note>'cause it's a jungle out there</Note>.]
> …蒙基辛啤酒。[MonkeyShine主题]MonkeyShine啤酒，因为外面是丛林。]
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,口语缩略">

- **'cause it's a jungle out there**：**'cause** = because 的口语缩略，引导**状语从句**（台：副词从句）表原因（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **out there**：副词短语"在外面（那个世界里）"；it's a jungle out there 是惯用语"外面可是弱肉强食"（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[Camera pans back from the TV to show the gang watching.]
> [摄像机从电视上向后平移，显示帮派正在观看。]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="9.48" duration="3.456" /> **ROSS GELLER:** That commercial always <Note>makes me so sad</Note>.
> 那个广告总是让我很难过。
<GrammarTip points="使役动词,基本句型">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">That commercial</span> <span class="v">makes</span> <span class="o">me</span> <span class="c">so sad</span></p>

- **make + 宾语 + 形容词补语**：S+V+O+C 句型，**宾语补足语**（台：补语）so sad 说明宾语 me 的状态——"使我这么难过"；make 表"使得"时不加 to（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **always**：频率副词置于实义动词前（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="12.936" duration="6.192" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Yeah, but then the guy opens his beer and those girls run at him, so, everything <Note>seems to work out</Note> OK.
> 是的，但那家伙打开啤酒，那些女孩朝他跑去，所以，一切似乎都很顺利。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,对等连接词,不定词">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="12.936" duration="6.192" />

**原句**：But then the guy opens his beer and those girls run at him, so everything seems to work out OK.

**层次划分**：

- 三个分句靠 **and / so** 串成合句：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">the guy</span> <span class="v">opens</span> <span class="o">his beer</span></p> + <p class="svoc"><span class="s">those girls</span> <span class="v">run</span> <span class="a">at him</span></p> + <p class="svoc"><span class="s">everything</span> <span class="v">seems</span> <span class="c">to work out OK</span></p>（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md) 对等连接词）
- **seems to work out OK**：seem 是**系动词**（台：连缀动词），后接**不定式**（台：不定词）to work out 作主语补语——"似乎进展顺利"；work out 短语动词"（事情）有了好结果"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- 叙事用**现在式**（opens/run/seems）——复述剧情时的"历史现在式"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

**翻译**：可是那家伙一打开啤酒，姑娘们就朝他奔去，所以嘛，一切似乎都很圆满。

**语法要点**：and/so 合句（Ch.15）；seem to do（Ch.4）；历史现在式（Ch.3）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="19.128" duration="3.84" /> **ROSS GELLER:** I meant <Note>because the monkey in it reminds me of Marcel</Note>.
> 我的意思是因为里面的猴子让我想起了马塞尔。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,介词,副词从句">

- **I meant...**：过去式"我刚才的意思是"——为自己刚才的话做解释时英语惯用过去式（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **because the monkey in it reminds me of Marcel**：because 引导原因**状语从句**（台：副词从句）（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **remind A of B**："使 A 想起 B"，**介词**（台：介系词）of 固定搭配；the monkey in it 中 in it 是介词短语作后置定语（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="22.968" duration="5.04" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** I can see that, 'cause they both have those big brown eyes and, ya know, <Note>the little pouty chin</Note>.
> 我看得出来，因为他们都有一双棕色的大眼睛，还有，你知道的，撅嘴的小下巴。
<GrammarTip points="形容词,插入语">

- **they both have**：both 作主语 they 的同位语，"他们俩都"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **big brown eyes**：多个形容词作定语的顺序——大小（big）在颜色（brown）前（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- **ya know**：= you know，插入语，前后用逗号隔开（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **the little pouty chin**：承接 eyes 的并列宾语，名词前省掉了 have；pouty"撅嘴的"（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="28.008" duration="2.856" /> **MONICA GELLER:** And <Note>the fact that they're both monkeys</Note>.
> 还有他们都是猴子的事实。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,省略">

- **the fact that they're both monkeys**：**that 引导同位语从句**（台：名词从句），补充说明 the fact 的内容——that 不作成分、不可换成 which（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- 整句是省略句 = And (there's) the fact that...，口语中承接上文只抛出名词词组（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="30.864" duration="4.848" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Sometimes I wonder <Note>if I did the right thing</Note>, ya know, <Note>giving him away</Note>.
> 有时候我想知道我是否做了正确的事，你知道，把他送人。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,名词从句,动名词">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="30.864" duration="4.848" />

**原句**：Sometimes I wonder if I did the right thing, ya know, giving him away.

**层次划分**：

- 主干：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">wonder</span> <span class="o">if I did the right thing</span></p>
- **if I did the right thing**：if（= whether"是否"）引导**宾语从句**（台：名词从句），从句用陈述语序、不倒装；主句 wonder 是现在式，从句按事实用过去式 did（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **giving him away**：**动名词**短语作主语补语性质的补充说明——"（我是说）把他送人这件事"；give away 短语动词"送掉、出让"（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

**翻译**：有时候我会想，把他送人，我做得对不对。

**语法要点**：wonder if + 陈述语序（Ch.12）；动名词短语作补充（Ch.5）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="35.712" duration="10.344" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Oh, Ross, <Note>you had to</Note>, I mean, <Note>he was humping everything in sight</Note>. I mean, I have a Malibu Barbi <Note>that will no longer be wearing white to her wedding</Note>.
> 哦，罗斯，你必须，我是说，他把眼前的一切都搞砸了。我的意思是，我有一个Malibu芭比娃娃，在她的婚礼上不再穿白色。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,关系从句,动词时态">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="35.712" duration="10.344" />

**原句**：You had to, I mean, he was humping everything in sight. I have a Malibu Barbi that will no longer be wearing white to her wedding.

**层次划分**：

- **you had to**：= you had to give him away，had to 是 have to"不得不"的过去式，后面动词因承接上文而省略（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **he was humping everything in sight**：过去进行式"（那时候）见什么骑什么"；in sight 介词短语作后置定语"视线所及的"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **a Malibu Barbi that will no longer be wearing white to her wedding**：**that 引导定语从句**（台：关系从句）修饰 Barbi，关系代词 that 在从句中作主语、不可省略；从句用**未来进行式** will be wearing 想象婚礼当天的画面——"不能再穿白纱出嫁了"（暗讽猴子毁了她）；no longer"不再"（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

**翻译**：罗斯，你别无选择，我是说，它见什么骑什么。我有个马里布芭比，被她害得婚礼那天再也没法穿白色了。

**语法要点**：have to 的省略（Ch.9）；关系代词作主语不可省（Ch.14）；will be doing（Ch.3）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="46.056" duration="6.696" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Remember <Note>when sometimes he'd borrow your hat</Note>, and, and when you got it back <Note>there'd be little monkey raisins in it</Note>.
> 还记得有时候他会借你的帽子吗？当你拿回来的时候，帽子里会有小葡萄干。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,名词从句,would">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="46.056" duration="6.696" />

**原句**：Remember when sometimes he'd borrow your hat, and when you got it back there'd be little monkey raisins in it.

**层次划分**：

- 主干是祈使句：<p class="svoc"><span class="v">Remember</span> <span class="o">when...raisins in it</span></p>
- **when sometimes he'd borrow your hat**：when 引导**宾语从句**（台：名词从句）作 remember 的宾语"还记得……的时候吗"；**he'd = he would**，would 表过去习惯性动作"他老是借"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **when you got it back**：第二个 when 从句是**状语从句**（台：副词从句）表时间（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **there'd be little monkey raisins in it**：**there'd = there would**，与上句 would 呼应——"（那时）里面就会有猴子屎蛋"；There be 存在句（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

**翻译**：还记得吗，他时不时借你的帽子，等你拿回来，里头总会有几粒猴子屎。

**语法要点**：remember + when 从句（Ch.12）；would 表过去惯常（Ch.9）；There would be（Ch.1）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="52.752" duration="9.384" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Yeah, well sure, <Note>when he did it, it was funny</Note>. When I did it to my boss's hat. . . <Note>all of the sudden</Note> I have this big attitude problem.
> 是的，当然，当他这么做的时候，很有趣。当我对老板的帽子下手的时候。突然之间，我的态度有了很大的问题。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,代词,惯用语">

- **when he did it, it was funny**：when 引导时间**状语从句**（台：副词从句）；从句的 it 指"往帽子里拉屎"这件事，主句的 it 是虚主语指整件事（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **When I did it to my boss's hat...**：句首大写 When 但全句未完——口语破句，后半句被情绪吞掉（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **all of the sudden**：惯用语正确写法是 **all of a sudden**"突然"，此处为转写变体（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **I have this big attitude problem**：叙述往事突然切换成**现在式**——历史现在式增强画面感；this 表"（不知哪来的）这么一个"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

OPENING TITLES
> 开场白

---

🎬 *Central Perk. Chandler, Monica, Rachel, and Phoebe are at the couch.*
> 🎬 *中央公园。钱德勒、莫妮卡、瑞秋和菲比坐在沙发上。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：名词从句 / would 的委婉与假设 / 过去进行式表委婉 / 短语动词

[Joey enters holding a letter]
> [乔伊拿着一封信进来]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="62.136" duration="3.648" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Hey, hey, <Note>check it out</Note>, <Note>guess what I got</Note>.
> 嘿，嘿，看看，猜猜我得到了什么。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词,名词从句">

- **check it out**：短语动词 check out"看看、瞧瞧"，代词 it 必须夹在动词与副词之间（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **guess what I got**：guess 后接 **what 引导的宾语从句**（台：名词从句），what 在从句中作 got 的宾语；口语常用祈使句 Guess what...卖关子（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="65.784" duration="1.464" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Rhythm?
> 节奏？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="67.248" duration="2.616" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** No, my first fan mail.
> 不，我的第一封粉丝来信。
<GrammarTip points="名词词组,省略">

- **my first fan mail**：省略句 = I got my first fan mail；fan mail"粉丝来信"为复合名词，mail 作"邮件"不可数（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="69.864" duration="0.816" /> **ALL:** Alright!
> 好的！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="70.68" duration="15.192" /> **MONICA GELLER:** [reading] 'Dear Dr. Remore, <Note>know that I love you</Note> and <Note>would do anything to have you</Note>.' Gosh. 'Your not-so-secretive admirer, Erica Ford.' Ooh wait, 'PS <Note>enclosed please find</Note> 14 of my eyelashes.'
> [阅读]“亲爱的雷莫尔医生，你知道我爱你，我愿意为你做任何事。”天哪你不那么神秘的仰慕者，艾丽卡·福特。哦，等等，请找到我的14根睫毛
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,名词从句,书信文体">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="70.68" duration="15.192" />

**原句**：Know that I love you and would do anything to have you. Enclosed please find 14 of my eyelashes.

**层次划分**：

- **know that I love you**：主句省略主语 (I)——书信/日记体常见；that 引导**宾语从句**（台：名词从句）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **would do anything to have you**：与 know 并列，共享被省略的主语 I；**would** 表意愿"愿意"；**to have you** 不定式（台：不定词）作目的状语"为了得到你"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **Enclosed please find 14 of my eyelashes**：商务书信套语 **Enclosed (,) please find...**"随信附上……"，是 With this letter you will find...enclosed 的凝缩；14 of my eyelashes"我的 14 根睫毛"——of 表所属（[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **your not-so-secretive admirer**：not-so- + 形容词"不那么……的"，复合修饰语带讽刺（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）

**翻译**：请知道我爱你，并愿意为得到你做任何事。随信附上我的十四根睫毛。

**语法要点**：书信省略主语（Ch.12/16）；would 表意愿（Ch.9）；Enclosed please find 套语

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="85.872" duration="3.768" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Ya know, in crazy world, <Note>that means you're married</Note>.
> 你们知道，在疯狂的世界里，这意味着你们结婚了。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">that</span> <span class="v">means</span> <span class="o">(that) you're married</span></p>

- **means (that) you're married**：宾语从句（台：名词从句）省略连接词 that——口语中 that 常省（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **in crazy world**：= in (the/a) crazy world，口语省略冠词（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="89.64" duration="9.12" /> **MONICA GELLER:** <Note>This wasn't addressed to</Note> Days of Our Lives, this is, this came to your apartment. <Note>There's no stamp on it</Note>, this woman was in our building.
> 这不是写给我们生活中的日子，这是，这是你的公寓。上面没有邮票，这个女人在我们的大楼里。
<GrammarTip points="被动语态,动词时态,推理">

- **This wasn't addressed to...**：be addressed to"（信）写给/寄给"，**过去式被动**；address 作动词"在（信封上）写姓名地址"（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）
- **There's no stamp on it**：There be 存在句；stamp 后的 on it 介词短语作后置定语（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **this woman was in our building**：Monica 全程用**过去式**做推理——"（这封信送来时）她人就在我们楼里"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="98.76" duration="3.312" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Oh my god, I got <Note>my very own stalker</Note>.
> 我的天啊，我有自己的跟踪狂。
<GrammarTip points="名词词组,强调">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">got</span> <span class="o">my very own stalker</span></p>

- **my very own + 名词**：very 作形容词"正是那个、十足的"，加强 own 的独占意味——"完完全全属于我自己的"；Joey 居然在炫耀（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- **got**：口语中 = have got"有了"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[Ross enters with a suitcase]
> [罗斯拿着手提箱进来]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="102.072" duration="1.8" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Hey guys.
> 嘿，伙计们。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="103.872" duration="0.744" /> **ALL:** Hey.
> 嘿。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="104.616" duration="2.28" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Ooh, <Note>where are you off to</Note>, Travelin' Jake?
> 哦，杰克，你要去哪里？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,短语动词">

- **Where are you off to?**：be off to + 地点"动身去……"；疑问词 where 提前、主谓倒装（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **Travelin' Jake**：起绰号——Travelin' = Traveling 的口语脱落尾音 g（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="106.896" duration="8.64" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Well, <Note>there's this, uh, paleontology conference in L.A.</Note> so <Note>I figured I'd go</Note> and then drive down to the zoo and surprise Marcel.
> 嗯，洛杉矶有一个古生物学会议。所以我想我应该去，然后开车去动物园给马塞尔一个惊喜。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,名词从句,would">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="106.896" duration="8.64" />

**原句**：There's this paleontology conference in L.A. so I figured I'd go and then drive down to the zoo and surprise Marcel.

**层次划分**：

- **there's this paleontology conference in L.A.**：There be 存在句 + **this** 引出话题——口语"有这么一个……"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- 主干：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">figured</span> <span class="o">(that) I'd go and drive down...and surprise Marcel</span></p> so 连接结果分句（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）
- **I'd go and (then) drive down to the zoo and surprise Marcel**：figured 后省略 that 的宾语从句；**I'd = I would**，would 此处表打算/设想；从句内 go / drive down / surprise 三个原形动词并列——"去→南下→给惊喜"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **drive down to**：短语动词"开车南下到……"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

**翻译**：洛杉矶有个古生物学研讨会，我打算开完会顺道开车去动物园，给马塞尔一个惊喜。

**语法要点**：I figured (that)...（Ch.12）；would 表打算（Ch.9）；三连动词并列（Ch.15）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="115.536" duration="8.232" /> **CHANDLER BING:** You know I think he will be surprised, <Note>'till he realizes he's a monkey</Note>, and uh, you know, <Note>isn't capable of that emotion</Note>.
> 你知道，我想他会感到惊讶的，“直到他意识到自己是只猴子，而且，呃，你知道，他无法表达那种情绪。”。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,副词从句,名词从句">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="115.536" duration="8.232" />

**原句**：I think he will be surprised, 'till he realizes he's a monkey and isn't capable of that emotion.

**层次划分**：

- 主干：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">think</span> <span class="o">(that) he will be surprised</span></p>（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **'till he realizes...**：**'till = until** 引导时间**状语从句**（台：副词从句）——"直到他意识到为止"；主句用 will 未来式、从句用**现在式 realizes 代未来**（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **realizes (that) he's a monkey and (that he) isn't capable of that emotion**：realize 后接省略 that 的宾语从句；从句内 he's 与 isn't 两个谓语并列共享主语 he；**be capable of + 名词**"有……的能力"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

**翻译**：他肯定会惊喜万分——直到他想起自己是只猴子，根本不具备那种情绪。

**语法要点**：until 从句现在式代未来（Ch.13）；realize + 宾语从句（Ch.12）；be capable of（Ch.10）

</GrammarTip>

[Rob (Chris Isaac) enters]
> [罗布（克里斯·艾萨克）进来]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="123.768" duration="4.008" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Oh, Phoebe, <Note>that really cute guy</Note> is here again.
> 哦，菲比，那个可爱的家伙又来了。
<GrammarTip points="形容词,副词">

- **that really cute guy**：程度副词 really 修饰形容词 cute，整体作 guy 的定语——"那个超可爱的男生"（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)/[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="127.776" duration="7.632" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh, oh, OK, so everyone, <Note>pretend like I'm telling you a story</Note>, OK. And, and it's really funny. So everyone just laugh, now.
> 哦，哦，好吧，大家都假装我在给你们讲故事，好吗。而且，这真的很有趣。所以现在大家都笑了。
<GrammarTip points="祈使句,副词从句">

- **pretend (like) I'm telling you a story**：祈使句；pretend 后可直接接从句，like 在此是口语化添头（= as if"好像"），引导方式**状语从句**（台：副词从句）——"假装我正在给你们讲故事"，从句用现在进行式表"正在进行"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **So everyone just laugh**：laugh 是祈使句原形动词；just"只管、尽管"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[Everyone laughs]
> [大家笑]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="135.408" duration="2.376" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** I know, I know. [to Rob] Hello.
> 我知道，我知道。[对罗布]你好。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="137.784" duration="1.896" /> **ROB:** Hi. I'm Rob Dohnen.
> 嗨。我是罗布·多恩。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="139.68" duration="1.464" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Hi Rob Dohnen.
> 嗨，罗布·多恩。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="141.144" duration="4.128" /> **ROB:** I don't know anything about music, but I think <Note>you're really, really great</Note>.
> 我对音乐一无所知，但我觉得你真的非常棒。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,对等连接词">

- **I don't know anything about music, but I think you're really, really great**：but 连接两个对等分句——先抑后扬的夸人公式（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）
- **think (that) you're...**：think 后省略 that 的宾语从句（台：名词从句）；really, really 重复加强语气（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="145.272" duration="0.888" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh, wow.
> 哦，哇。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="146.16" duration="8.688" /> **ROB:** Anyway, I schedule performers for the <Note>childrens libraries</Note> around the city and <Note>I was just thinking</Note>, <Note>have you ever thought about playing your songs for kids</Note>?
> 不管怎样，我为全市的儿童图书馆安排了表演者，我在想，你有没有想过为孩子们播放你的歌曲？
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,动词时态,动名词">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="146.16" duration="8.688" />

**原句**：I schedule performers for the childrens libraries around the city and I was just thinking, have you ever thought about playing your songs for kids?

**层次划分**：

- <p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">schedule</span> <span class="o">performers</span> <span class="a">for the childrens libraries around the city</span></p> schedule 作动词"为……排日程"；around the city 介词短语后置定语（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **I was just thinking...**：**过去进行式表委婉**——"我刚才随口一想"，比 I think 更试探、更不唐突（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **have you ever thought about playing your songs for kids?**：**现在完成式 + ever**"有没有想过"；about 是介词，后接**动名词** playing（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）

**翻译**：我负责全市儿童图书馆的演出安排，刚才在想：你有没有想过给小朋友唱你的歌？

**语法要点**：过去进行式表委婉（Ch.3）；think about + V-ing（Ch.5）

**原文勘误**：`childrens libraries` 应为 **children's libraries**（漏所有格撇号）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="154.848" duration="5.496" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh, <Note>I would love to have kids</Note>. . . you're, you're the, you're, me play the songs <Note>that I will write for them</Note>.
> 哦，我很想有孩子。你是，你是，你是，我播放我将为他们写的歌曲。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,关系从句">

- **I would love to have kids**：**would love to + 原形**"很乐意……"；Phoebe 故意误听——Rob 说的是 sing **for** kids，她接成 have kids（要孩子），制造笑点（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **the songs that I will write for them**：**that 引导定语从句**（台：关系从句）修饰 songs；that 在从句中作 write 的宾语，**可以省略**（= the songs I will write for them）（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Chandler and Joey's apartment. Chandler and Joey are standing around in the kitchen.*
> 🎬 *钱德勒和乔伊的公寓。钱德勒和乔伊站在厨房周围。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：口语缩略 / 动名词 / in case 状语从句 / 时间从句现在式代未来

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="160.344" duration="2.976" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Hey, <Note>whaddya wanna do</Note> for dinner?
> 嘿，你晚餐想吃什么？
<GrammarTip points="口语缩略,不定词">

- **whaddya wanna do**：三层缩略——whaddya = what do you；wanna = want to；want to do 中 **to do 是不定式**（台：不定词）作 want 的宾语（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **for dinner**：for 表"（哪一餐）吃什么"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="163.32" duration="6.024" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Well <Note>we could just stay in and cook for ourselves</Note>. [both laugh hysterically]
> 我们可以呆在家里自己做饭。[两人都歇斯底里地笑]
<GrammarTip points="情态动词,短语动词,反身代词">

- **could + 原形**：could 表提议"我们可以……"（比 can 更委婉）（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **stay in**：短语动词"宅在家"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **cook for ourselves**：反身代词 ourselves 与主语 we 呼应"自己（动手）做"——说完两人狂笑，因为这俩人从不开火（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[door buzzer goes off]
> [门蜂鸣器响]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="169.344" duration="1.512" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Hello.
> 你好。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="170.856" duration="1.272" /> **ERICA:** It's Erica.
> 是艾丽卡。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="172.128" duration="2.28" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Ah, the stalker.
> 啊，跟踪者。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="174.408" duration="1.56" /> **ERICA:** Never mind, it's open.
> 没关系，它是开着的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="175.968" duration="10.92" /> **CHANDLER BING:** [Joey grabs a frying pan] Yes, <Note>hitting her with a frying pan's a good idea</Note>. <Note>We might wanna have a backup plan</Note>, though, <Note>just in case she isn't a cartoon</Note>.
> （乔伊拿起煎锅）是的，用煎锅打她是个好主意。不过，我们可能需要一个后备计划，以防她不是卡通人物。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,动名词,副词从句">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="175.968" duration="10.92" />

**原句**：Hitting her with a frying pan's a good idea. We might wanna have a backup plan, though, just in case she isn't a cartoon.

**层次划分**：

- <p class="svoc"><span class="s">Hitting her with a frying pan</span> <span class="v">'s</span> <span class="c">a good idea</span></p> **动名词短语作主语**——"拿平底锅拍她这件事是个好主意"；with a frying pan 介词短语表工具（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **We might wanna have a backup plan**：**might** 表不确定的建议"或许该"；wanna = want to（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **just in case she isn't a cartoon**：**in case** 引导目的/防备**状语从句**（台：副词从句）"以防万一"；从句用**现在式**（isn't）——Chandler 毒舌：万一她不是卡通人物，一平底锅可拍不晕（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **though**：副词"不过"，置于句尾作转承（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

**翻译**：拿平底锅拍她，这主意不错。不过咱们最好留个后手——万一她不是卡通人物呢。

**语法要点**：动名词短语作主语（Ch.5）；in case + 现在式（Ch.13）；句尾 though（Ch.8）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="186.888" duration="2.208" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** <Note>Let's get out of here</Note>.
> 我们离开这里吧。
<GrammarTip points="祈使句,短语动词">

- **Let's + 原形**：Let us 的缩略，提议"咱们……吧"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **get out of here**：短语动词"离开这儿"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[They run out and knock on Monica and Rachel's door]
> [他们跑出去敲莫妮卡和瑞秋的门]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="189.096" duration="2.52" /> **CHANDLER BING:** <Note>The one time</Note> they're not home.
> 有一次他们不在家。
<GrammarTip points="省略,关系从句">

- **The one time (that) they're not home**：省略感叹句——"(Of all times,) the one time..."；the one time 后接省略关系副词 when/that 的**定语从句**（台：关系从句）——"偏偏就这一次他们不在家"（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="191.616" duration="5.928" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** OK, we'll just leave, and <Note>when we pass her on the stairs</Note>, she won't know it's me <Note>'cause we've never met</Note>.
> 好吧，我们就走吧，当我们在楼梯上从她身边经过时，她不会知道是我，因为我们从未见过面。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,副词从句,动词时态">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="191.616" duration="5.928" />

**原句**：We'll just leave, and when we pass her on the stairs, she won't know it's me 'cause we've never met.

**层次划分**：

- 主干：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">we</span> <span class="v">'ll leave</span></p> will 表决定"我们就走"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **when we pass her on the stairs**：when 时间**状语从句**（台：副词从句），**现在式 pass 代未来**（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **she won't know (that) it's me**：主句 won't know 未来式，与从句现在式呼应；know 后接省略 that 的宾语从句（台：名词从句）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **'cause we've never met**：'cause = because 原因从句；**现在完成式 have never met**"至今从未见过"——正是她能认出他的理由（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

**翻译**：咱们直接走人，等会儿在楼梯上跟她擦肩而过，她也认不出是我——咱俩从没见过面嘛。

**语法要点**：when 从句现在式代未来（Ch.13）；完成式 + never（Ch.3）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="197.544" duration="3.408" /> **CHANDLER BING:** <Note>That's how radio stars escape stalkers</Note>.
> 这就是广播明星逃避跟踪者的方式。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,讽刺">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">That</span> <span class="v">'s</span> <span class="c">how radio stars escape stalkers</span></p>

- **how radio stars escape stalkers**：how 引导**表语从句**（台：名词从句）——"广播明星就是这样甩掉跟踪狂的"；从句用陈述语序（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- Chandler 讽刺 Joey"我们没见过面所以她认不出我"这套逻辑只适用于不露脸的广播明星

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="200.952" duration="1.848" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** She's comin'.
> 她来了。

[Both run back in their apartment. There's a knock at the door.]
> [两人都跑回公寓。有人敲门。]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="202.8" duration="0.864" /> **ERICA:** It's me.
> 是我。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="203.664" duration="5.04" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Uhh, this is it, <Note>this is how we're gonna die</Note>. Ready?
> 嗯，就是这样，我们就这样死去。准备好的
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,将来时">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">this</span> <span class="v">is</span> <span class="c">how we're gonna die</span></p>

- **how we're gonna die**：how 引导表语从句（台：名词从句）"我们就是这样死的"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **gonna** = going to——be going to 表"即将发生"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **this is it**：惯用语"（关键时刻/大限）到了"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="208.704" duration="5.856" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Wait, wait, wait. [Opens the top of the dish soap he's holding]
> 等等，等等，等等。[打开他拿着的肥皂的顶部]

[Joey opens the door and sees Erica (Brooke Shields). Joey gets a huge smile and Chandler squeezed the dish soap in the air.]
> [乔伊打开门，看到艾丽卡（布鲁克·希尔兹饰）。乔伊笑得很开心，钱德勒把香皂挤在空中。]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="214.56" duration="0.768" /> **ERICA:** Hi.
> 嗨。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="215.328" duration="1.68" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Erica.
> 艾丽卡。

---

🎬 *San Diego Zoo. Ross is at the Monkey cages.*
> 🎬 *圣地亚哥动物园。罗斯在猴笼里。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：被动语态 / should have + 过去分词 / 委婉语 / 比较级

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="217.008" duration="5.376" /> **LIPSON:** Hi, Dean Lipson, zoo administrator. <Note>I was told you had a question</Note>.
> 嗨，迪安·利普森，动物园管理员。我听说你有个问题。
<GrammarTip points="被动语态,名词从句,同位语">

- **Dean Lipson, zoo administrator**：同位语——职位头衔直接跟在姓名后补充说明（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **I was told (that) you had a question**：**被动语态** was told"有人告诉我"；tell 后原接双宾语（tell sb sth），被动化后 sth（此处为省略 that 的宾语从句）留下；从句用过去式 had 与主句时态呼应（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="222.384" duration="8.592" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Well, I uh, <Note>I can't seem to find the monkey I donated last year</Note>. He's a capuchan, <Note>answers to the name Marcel</Note>.
> 嗯，我好像找不到我去年捐赠的猴子了。他是一个卷尾猴，名字叫马塞尔。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,关系从句,短语动词">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="222.384" duration="8.592" />

**原句**：I can't seem to find the monkey I donated last year. He's a capuchan, answers to the name Marcel.

**层次划分**：

- 主干：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">can't seem to find</span> <span class="o">the monkey</span></p> **can't seem to do**"好像怎么也……"——seem 后接不定式（台：不定词）to find（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **the monkey (that) I donated last year**：**定语从句**（台：关系从句）修饰 monkey；关系代词 that/which 在从句中作 donated 的宾语，**故可省略**（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）
- **(he) answers to the name Marcel**：省略主语；**answer to the name...**"（动物）听到……这个名字有反应"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

**翻译**：呃，我好像找不到我去年捐的那只猴子了。它是只卷尾猴，叫马塞尔有反应。

**语法要点**：can't seem to do（Ch.4）；关系代词作宾语可省（Ch.14）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="230.976" duration="3.984" /> **LIPSON:** Ahh, <Note>I'm afraid I have some bad news</Note>. Marcel <Note>has passed on</Note>.
> 啊，恐怕我有一些坏消息。马塞尔去世了。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,动词时态,委婉语">

- **I'm afraid (that) I have some bad news**：I'm afraid + 宾语从句（台：名词从句）——报坏消息前的缓冲句式"恐怕……"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **has passed on**：**现在完成式**宣布"刚刚发生、与现在相关"的事；**pass on** = die 的委婉说法（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="234.96" duration="2.712" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Oh my God, what happened?
> 天啊，发生什么事了？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="237.672" duration="4.824" /> **LIPSON:** Well he got sick, and then he got <Note>sicker</Note>, and then he got <Note>a little better</Note> but then he died.
> 他病了，然后病得更重，然后他好了一点，但后来他死了。
<GrammarTip points="形容词比较,对等连接词">

- **got sick → got sicker → got a little better**：get + 形容词表状态变化；**sicker / better** 都是比较级——病情一波三折全靠比较级推进（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- **and then...and then...but then**：and/but 对等连接词串起排比，口语叙事的节奏感（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="242.496" duration="2.232" /> **ROSS GELLER:** I can't believe this.
> 我真不敢相信。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="244.728" duration="8.832" /> **LIPSON:** I'm sorry Mr. Geller. But ya know, <Note>there's an old saying</Note>, 'Sometimes monkeys die.' It's not a great saying but it certainly <Note>is fitting</Note> today.
> 对不起，盖勒先生。但你知道，有句老话，“有时猴子会死。”这不是一句伟大的谚语，但它在今天确实是合适的。
<GrammarTip points="存在句,同位语,强调">

- **there's an old saying, 'Sometimes monkeys die.'**：There be 存在句，引语 'Sometimes monkeys die' 作 saying 的同位语（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **it certainly is fitting**：certainly 加强语气；**is** 重读表强调"确实很应景"——助动词/系动词重读即强调（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="253.56" duration="3.576" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Well, ya know, <Note>someone should have called me</Note>.
> 嗯，你知道，应该有人给我打电话。
<GrammarTip points="情态动词,虚拟">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">someone</span> <span class="v">should have called</span> <span class="o">me</span></p>

- **should have + 过去分词**："（当时）本该……（却没做）"——对过去的责备/遗憾；此处是被动意味的主动表达 = I should have been called（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="257.136" duration="5.184" /> **LIPSON:** I'm sorry. Look, <Note>I know this can't bring him back</Note> but here, it's just a gesture.
> 对不起。听着，我知道这不能让他回来，但在这里，这只是一种姿态。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,情态动词">

- **I know (that) this can't bring him back**：know 后省略 that 的宾语从句（台：名词从句）；**can't** 表能力"不可能让他起死回生"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="262.32" duration="1.896" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Zoo dollars?
> 动物园美元？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="264.216" duration="7.944" /> **LIPSON:** Yes, and <Note>come see the bird show at 4</Note>. The macaws wear hats. Well <Note>it's a lot cuter if your monkey hasn't just died</Note>.
> 是的，4点来看鸟展。金刚鹦鹉戴帽子。如果你的猴子不是刚死的话，那就更可爱了。
<GrammarTip points="祈使句,形容词比较,副词从句">

- **come see the bird show**：口语 **come + 原形**（= come and see）"来看……"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **it's a lot cuter**：**a lot + 比较级**"可爱得多"（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **if your monkey hasn't just died**：if 条件**状语从句**（台：副词从句）；**现在完成式 + just**"刚死没多久"——黑色幽默：您的猴子刚死，这演出对您就没那么可爱了（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Library. Phoebe is getting ready to sing for the kids. Chandler, Monica, and Rachel are there.*
> 🎬 *图书馆。菲比准备为孩子们唱歌。钱德勒、莫妮卡和瑞秋在那里。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：宾语从句 / be used to / 定语从句 / 完成式与完成进行式

[Phoebe pulls out her guitar]
> [菲比拿出她的吉他]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="272.16" duration="0.744" /> **KIDS:** Ooohhh.
> 哦。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="272.904" duration="0.864" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** I know.
> 我知道。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="273.768" duration="6.456" /> **MONICA GELLER:** <Note>I can't believe Joey's having lunch with his stalker</Note>. What i-, <Note>what is she like</Note>.
> 真不敢相信乔伊居然和他的跟踪者一起吃午饭。我，她长什么样。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,动词时态,疑问句">

- **I can't believe (that) Joey's having lunch with his stalker**：believe 后省略 that 的宾语从句；从句用**现在进行式** is having——"（这会儿）正在跟跟踪狂吃午饭"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **What is she like?**：**What's sb like?**"这人什么样？"——like 是介词"像"，不是动词"喜欢"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="280.224" duration="3.432" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Well, you remember Cathy Bates in Misery?
> 嗯，你还记得凯西·贝茨在痛苦中吗？

RACHEL AND **MONICA GELLER: **Yeah.

> 瑞秋和**莫妮卡·盖勒：**是的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="283.656" duration="3.552" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Well, she looks <Note>the exact opposite of that</Note>.
> 嗯，她看起来正好相反。
<GrammarTip points="系动词,名词词组">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">she</span> <span class="v">looks</span> <span class="c">the exact opposite of that</span></p>

- **look + 补语**：look 作**系动词**（台：连缀动词）"看起来"，后接名词词组作补语（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **the exact opposite of that**："跟那个（凯西·贝茨）完全相反"——exact 加强 opposite；opposite of...固定搭配（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="287.208" duration="2.16" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** And she's not crazy?
> 她没有疯吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="289.368" duration="8.064" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Oh no no no, she's a total wack job. Yeah, <Note>she thinks that Joey is actually Dr. Drake Remore</Note>.
> 哦，不，不，她是个十足的怪人。是的，她认为乔伊实际上是德雷克·雷莫尔医生。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

- **she thinks (that) Joey is actually Dr. Drake Remore**：think + 宾语从句（台：名词从句）；actually 副词"真的（本人）就是"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **a total wack job**：口语"十足的疯子"，total 加强语气（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="297.432" duration="1.776" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Oh my God.
> 我的天啊。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="299.208" duration="5.472" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Ah, and I mean, <Note>he's going out with her</Note>? He can not <Note>persue</Note> this.
> 啊，我是说，他要和她约会？他无法理解这一点。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,情态动词">

- **he's going out with her?**：**现在进行式表将来**——"他真要跟她约会？"；go out with sb"与某人约会"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **He cannot persue this**：cannot + 原形"不能任由这事发展下去"；**原文勘误**：`persue` 应为 **pursue**（拼写漏 u）（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="304.68" duration="18.264" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Hey, <Note>just because this woman thinks she can actually see Joey through the magical box in her living room doesn't mean she's not a person</Note>. I mean, <Note>does she not deserve happiness, does she not deserve love</Note>? <Note>What're you lookin' at me for</Note>? He's the one <Note>who wants to boff the maniac</Note>.
> 嘿，仅仅因为这个女人认为她可以通过客厅里的魔法盒子看到乔伊，并不意味着她不是一个人。我的意思是，她不值得幸福，她不值得爱吗？你为什么看着我？他就是那个想摆脱疯子的人。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,副词从句,关系从句">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="304.68" duration="18.264" />

**原句**：Just because this woman thinks she can actually see Joey through the magical box in her living room doesn't mean she's not a person. Does she not deserve happiness, does she not deserve love? What're you lookin' at me for? He's the one who wants to boff the maniac.

**层次划分**：

- **Just because...doesn't mean...**：**because 原因状语从句（台：副词从句）整段作主句 doesn't mean 的主语**——"仅仅因为……并不意味着……"；从句内又套 thinks (that) she can see...两层宾语从句（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **does she not deserve happiness, does she not deserve love?**：否定疑问句作**修辞问句**——"她难道不配幸福、不配被爱吗？"，重复排比加强反问（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **What're you lookin' at me for?**：= What are you looking at me for?——**What...for?** = Why"干吗……"；look at 短语动词，疑问词 what 本是介词 for 的宾语（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- **the one who wants to boff the maniac**：**who 引导定语从句**（台：关系从句）修饰 the one，who 作主语不可省；boff 俚语"搞、上"（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

**翻译**：不就因为她以为自己真能透过客厅里那个魔法盒子看见乔伊嘛，这并不代表她不是个人。她难道不配幸福？不配被爱？你们瞪我干吗？想泡那个疯子的又不是我！

**语法要点**：Just because...doesn't mean（Ch.13）；否定疑问修辞（Ch.9）；What...for（Ch.10）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="322.944" duration="1.056" /> **ROB:** You OK?
> 你还好吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="324.0" duration="6.096" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** No, uh-uh, I'm just, I'm nervous. So, you know what, maybe <Note>if I just, if I picture them all in their underwear</Note>.
> 不，我只是，我很紧张。所以，你知道吗，如果我只是，如果我想象他们都穿着内衣。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句">

- **if I picture them all in their underwear**：if 条件**状语从句**（台：副词从句）——句子没说完，主句（"我就能放松"）被咽回去；picture 作动词"想象"；in their underwear"只穿内衣"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="330.096" duration="3.792" /> **ROB:** That's not a good idea, <Note>that's kinda the reason the last guy got fired</Note>.
> 这不是个好主意，这就是最后一个人被解雇的原因。
<GrammarTip points="关系从句,被动语态">

- **the reason (why/that) the last guy got fired**：**定语从句**（台：关系从句）修饰 reason，关系副词 why/that 常省略（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）
- **got fired**：**get + 过去分词** 表被动"被炒鱿鱼"，比 was fired 更口语（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）
- **kinda** = kind of"有点儿"（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="333.888" duration="12.192" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** I'm just, I'm, I'm, <Note>I'm used to playing for grown-ups</Note>. Ya know, they just, grown-ups drink their coffee and do their grown-up thing, ya know, and kids listen. This is a huge responsibility. What? <Note>Are you gonna kiss me</Note>?
> 我只是，我，我，我习惯了为成年人踢球。你知道，他们只是，大人喝他们的咖啡，做他们长大后的事，你知道，孩子们听着。这是一个巨大的责任。什么？你要吻我吗？
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,动名词,将来时">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="333.888" duration="12.192" />

**原句**：I'm used to playing for grown-ups. Grown-ups drink their coffee and do their grown-up thing, and kids listen. This is a huge responsibility. Are you gonna kiss me?

**层次划分**：

- **I'm used to playing for grown-ups**：**be used to + 动名词**"习惯于……"——to 是介词，后接 V-ing（≠ used to + 原形"过去常常"）（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **grown-ups drink their coffee and do their grown-up thing, and kids listen**：三组 S+V 平行排比，and 连接（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）
- **Are you gonna kiss me?**：**gonna = going to**，be going to 表"正打算"——Phoebe 突然戳破 Rob 的心思（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

**翻译**：我习惯给大人演出。大人嘛，喝喝咖啡、忙大人的事，孩子只管听。这可是天大的责任。怎么？你想亲我？

**语法要点**：be used to V-ing ≠ used to do（Ch.5）；be going to（Ch.3）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="346.08" duration="2.328" /> **ROSS GELLER:** <Note>I was thinkin' about it</Note>.
> 我在想这件事。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

- **I was thinking about it**：**过去进行式**——此处既委婉又俏皮："（你别说，）我刚还真动过这念头"；think about + 名词/动名词"考虑"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="348.408" duration="6.312" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** OK. [they kiss] OK, alrighty, let's play some tunes. Hi everybody, I'm Phoebe
> 好的。[他们接吻]好的，好的，让我们演奏一些曲子。大家好，我是菲比

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="354.72" duration="1.272" /> **ALL:** Hi Phoebe.
> 大家好，菲比。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="355.992" duration="5.136" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** OK, um, I'm gonna play, um, some songs about grandparents, OK. [singing]
> 好的，嗯，我要放一些关于祖父母的歌，好的。[唱]

Now, grandma's a person <Note>who everyone likes</Note>,
> 奶奶是一个大家都喜欢的人，
<GrammarTip points="关系从句">

- **a person who everyone likes**：**who 引导定语从句**（台：关系从句）修饰 person；who 在从句中作 likes 的**宾语**（= whom，口语常用 who）——"人人都喜欢的那种人"（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

she bought you a train and a bright, shiny bike.
> 她给你买了一辆火车和一辆闪亮的自行车。
<GrammarTip points="基本句型">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">she</span> <span class="v">bought</span> <span class="o">you</span> <span class="o">a train and a bright, shiny bike</span></p>

- **buy + 间接宾语 + 直接宾语**：S+V+O+O 双宾语句型"给你买……"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

But lately she <Note>hasn't been coming</Note> to dinner,
> 但最近她没来吃饭，
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

- **hasn't been coming**：**现在完成进行式**——"最近这段日子一直都没来吃饭"，lately 是完成式标志性副词（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

And last time you saw her she looked <Note>so much thinner</Note>.
> 上次你见到她时，她看起来瘦多了。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,形容词比较">

- **last time you saw her**：last time + 从句 = 时间**状语从句**（台：副词从句）"上次你见她的时候"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **so much thinner**：**so much + 比较级**"瘦了那么多"（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Now, your mom and your dad said she moved to Peru,
> 你爸妈说她搬到秘鲁去了，

but <Note>the truth is she died and some day you will too</Note>.
> 但事实是她死了，总有一天你也会死的。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,省略">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">the truth</span> <span class="v">is</span> <span class="c">(that) she died and some day you will (die) too</span></p>

- **the truth is (that)...**：is 后接**表语从句**（台：名词从句），that 省略；从句内 and 连接两个分句（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **some day you will too**：**will 后省略 die**——避免重复，too"也（会死）"（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

</GrammarTip>

La-la-la la la-la-la la la-la-la la...
> 啦啦啦啦啦啦啦啦啦。。。

---

🎬 *A nice restraunt. Joey and Erica are dining.*
> 🎬 *一位漂亮的姑妈。乔伊和艾丽卡正在吃饭。*

<GrammarTip points="原文勘误">

- ⚠️ 场景描述原文 `restraunt` 应为 **restaurant**（拼写漏 au）；中译"姑妈"为 **restaurant** 的机翻错误，应为"餐厅"

</GrammarTip>

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：否定疑问句 / 过去进行式 / 虚拟夸张 / 最高级

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="361.128" duration="2.016" /> **ERICA:** Oh, Drake, <Note>isn't it amazing</Note>?
> 哦，德雷克，这不是很神奇吗？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,语气">

- **isn't it amazing?**：否定疑问句表感叹——"这岂不妙哉？"；期待对方认同（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="363.144" duration="2.088" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Yeah it is. . . what?
> 是的，它是。什么？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="365.232" duration="6.888" /> **ERICA:** Well, <Note>here we sit</Note>, <Note>devil may care</Note>, just a little while ago <Note>you were reattaching someone's spinal cord</Note>.
> 好吧，我们坐在这里，魔鬼可能会在乎，就在刚才你还在给别人的脊髓重新连接。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,倒装,动词时态">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="365.232" duration="6.888" />

**原句**：Here we sit, devil may care, just a little while ago you were reattaching someone's spinal cord.

**层次划分**：

- **here we sit**：地点副词 here 置句首的**半倒装**——主语是代词 we 时主谓不倒装（对比 Here comes the bus）（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- **devil-may-care**：固定形容词"漫不经心的、潇洒的"——此处作插入语描绘氛围（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- **just a little while ago you were reattaching someone's spinal cord**：**过去进行式**——"就在刚才你还在给人家接脊椎"，ago 短语前置于句首作时间状语（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

**翻译**：我们竟能这样悠闲地坐在这儿——要知道，片刻之前你还在给病人重接脊椎呢。

**语法要点**：here 句首半倒装（Ch.22）；过去进行式 + ago（Ch.3）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="372.12" duration="8.52" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Yeah, that was a tricky one. In reality, that operation <Note>takes like, over 10 hours</Note>, but they only showed it for 2 <Note>minites</Note>.
> 是的，那是个棘手的问题。实际上，这个手术需要10个多小时，但他们只展示了2分钟。
<GrammarTip points="动词用法,对等连接词">

- **that operation takes over 10 hours**：**take** 表"（事情）花费（时间）"——主语是事物（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **like**：口语填充词"大概、像"（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **but they only showed it for 2 minutes**：but 转折；**原文勘误**：`minites` 应为 **minutes**（字母颠倒）（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="380.64" duration="0.96" /> **ERICA:** Who's they?
> 他们是谁？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="381.6" duration="1.776" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** No one.
> 没有人。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="383.376" duration="9.192" /> **ERICA:** Oh Drake, you are so talented, let me see those hands. Oh these hands, these beautiful hands, oh <Note>I could just eat them</Note>. . . <Note>but I won't</Note>.
> 哦，德雷克，你太有天赋了，让我看看你的手。哦，这些手，这些漂亮的手，哦，我可以把它们吃掉。但我不会。
<GrammarTip points="情态动词,省略">

- **I could just eat them**：**could** 表夸张的假设——"我简直能把它们吃了"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **but I won't**：**won't = will not**，后省略 eat them——"但我不会（真吃）"（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）
- **let me see those hands**：使役动词 let + 宾语 + 原形补语（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="392.568" duration="5.256" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Good, <Note>otherwise my watch would fall off</Note>. [laughs hysterically]
> 很好，否则我的手表会掉下来。[歇斯底里地笑]
<GrammarTip points="虚拟语气,副词">

- **otherwise my watch would fall off**：**otherwise**"否则"引出的假设——(if you ate them,) my watch **would** fall off；would 表与事实相反的虚拟（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **fall off**：短语动词"脱落"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="397.824" duration="9.984" /> **ERICA:** No, seriously. These hands. These miracle, magical, <Note>life-giving hands</Note>. Oh, <Note>just to be near them, touch them, maybe even lick one</Note>?
> 不，说真的。这些手。这些奇迹，神奇，赋予生命的手。哦，就为了靠近他们，摸摸他们，甚至舔一只？
<GrammarTip points="复合形容词,不定词">

- **life-giving hands**：复合形容词"赋予生命的"——名词 + 现在分词构成（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)/[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）
- **just to be near them, (to) touch them, (to) maybe even lick one**：三个**不定式**（台：不定词）并列作目的状语，后两个省略 to——"就为了能挨着它们、摸摸它们、甚至舔上一口"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="407.808" duration="6.648" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Alright, just one. [she licks his hands rather emphatically] Wow, <Note>you're good at that</Note>.
> 好的，就一个。[她用力舔了舔他的手]哇，你很擅长这个。
<GrammarTip points="介词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">'re</span> <span class="c">good at that</span></p>

- **be good at + 名词/动名词**："擅长……"，at 是介词（台：介系词）（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[Some guy at another table starts choking]
> [另一张桌子上的人开始窒息]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="414.456" duration="3.84" /> **WAITER:** Oh my God! Someone, <Note>he's choking</Note>. Is anyone here a doctor?
> 哦，天哪！有人，他噎住了。这里有医生吗？
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,存在句">

- **he's choking**：**现在进行式**"（这会儿正）噎着"——紧急情况用进行式（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **Is anyone here a doctor?**：疑问句中 anyone 作主语，**主谓一致**（台：主语动词一致性）用单数 is（[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="418.296" duration="4.176" /> **ERICA:** Well, yes, yes, <Note>the best doctor in all of Salem</Note>, Dr. Drake Remore.
> 是的，是的，塞勒姆最好的医生，德雷克·雷莫尔医生。
<GrammarTip points="形容词比较,同位语">

- **the best doctor in all of Salem**：**最高级 + in + 范围**——"全塞勒姆最好的医生"（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- **Dr. Drake Remore**：同位语，点明"最好的医生"是谁（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *San Diege Zoo. Ross is still at the monkey cages. A janitor is sweeping.*
> 🎬 *圣地亚哥动物园。罗斯仍在猴笼里。一名看门人正在清扫。*

<GrammarTip points="原文勘误">

- ⚠️ 场景描述原文 `San Diege` 应为 **San Diego**（拼写多 e）

</GrammarTip>

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：祈使句 / enjoy + 动名词 / 补语形容词

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="422.472" duration="3.336" /> **JANITOR:** <Note>Meet me in the nocturnal house in 15 minutes</Note>.
> 15分钟后在夜行屋见我。
<GrammarTip points="祈使句,介词">

- **Meet me...**：祈使句省略主语 you（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **in the nocturnal house / in 15 minutes**：两个 in 含义不同——前者表地点"在夜行动物馆"，后者表"……之后"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="425.808" duration="7.488" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Uhh, hey look, <Note>I don't really enjoy being with other men that way</Note>. But, um, zoo dollars?
> 嗯，嘿，听着，我真的不喜欢这样和其他男人在一起。但是，嗯，动物园的钱？
<GrammarTip points="动名词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">don't enjoy</span> <span class="o">being with other men that way</span></p>

- **enjoy + 动名词**：enjoy 后只能接 **V-ing**——enjoy being with..."喜欢与……相处"；Ross 误会清洁工在约他（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）
- **that way**：方式状语"以那种方式"（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="433.296" duration="2.328" /> **JANITOR:** It's about your monkey. <Note>It's alive</Note>.
> 是关于你的猴子的。它还活着。
<GrammarTip points="补语">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">It</span> <span class="v">'s</span> <span class="c">alive</span></p>

- **alive**：作主语补语（表语）的形容词——"还活着"；alive 只能作补语、不能放名词前（× an alive monkey → a live monkey）（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Restraunt. Joey and Erica are still there.*
> 🎬 *阿姨。乔伊和艾丽卡还在那里。*

<GrammarTip points="原文勘误">

- ⚠️ 场景描述原文 `Restraunt` 应为 **restaurant**；中译"阿姨"同为机翻错误

</GrammarTip>

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：don't have to / 反身代词 / who am I to do / 惯用语

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="435.624" duration="2.88" /> **ERICA:** I don't understand, <Note>why didn't you help that man</Note>?
> 我不明白，你为什么不帮助那个人？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,动词时态">

- **why didn't you help that man?**：**过去式否定疑问**——"（刚才）你为什么不出手救他？"，didn't 把动作定位在那一刻（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="438.504" duration="9.792" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Uhh, cause, uhh, I'm a neurosurgeon and <Note>that was clearly a case of, uh, uh, foodal chokage</Note>. Alright, look, <Note>I got to tell you something</Note>.
> 嗯，因为，嗯，我是神经外科医生，那显然是一个，嗯，嗯，食物阻塞的病例。好吧，听着，我得告诉你一件事。
<GrammarTip points="名词词组,情态动词">

- **a case of + 名词**："一例……（病例）"；foodal chokage 是 Joey 现场瞎编的"医学术语"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **I got to tell you something**：= **I've got to** = I have to"我得……"；got to 后接不定式（台：不定词）to tell（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="448.296" duration="8.664" /> **ERICA:** No, no no no, <Note>you don't have to tell me anything</Note>. <Note>You don't have to explain yourself to me</Note>. Ooh, <Note>who am I to question the great Dr. Drake Remore</Note>?
> 不，不，不，你什么都不用告诉我。你不必向我解释你自己。哦，我是谁来质疑伟大的德雷克·雷莫尔博士？
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,情态动词,惯用语">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="448.296" duration="8.664" />

**原句**：You don't have to tell me anything. You don't have to explain yourself to me. Who am I to question the great Dr. Drake Remore?

**层次划分**：

- **you don't have to tell me anything**：**don't have to = 不必**（≠ must not"不准"）；tell + 双宾语 tell sb sth（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **explain yourself to me**：**反身代词 yourself** 与主语 you 呼应；explain sth/sb to sb"向某人解释"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **Who am I to question the great Dr. Drake Remore?**：惯用句式 **Who am I to do...?**"我算老几，哪能……"——不定式 to question 作后置定语；疑问词 who 提前倒装（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

**翻译**：你什么都不必跟我说，不必向我解释。我是谁呀，哪敢质疑伟大的德雷克·雷莫尔医生？

**语法要点**：don't have to ≠ must not（Ch.9）；Who am I to do（Ch.4/22）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="456.96" duration="1.848" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** But that's what...
> 但那就是。。。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="458.808" duration="1.992" /> **ERICA:** <Note>I should just be happy to be near you</Note>.
> 我应该很高兴能靠近你。
<GrammarTip points="情态动词,不定词">

- **should + 原形**：should 表"理应"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **be happy to be near you**：**不定式**（台：不定词）to be near you 作原因状语——"能待在你身边就（该）知足了"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="460.8" duration="3.096" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Hey I- [she cuts him off with a kiss]
> 嘿，我-[她吻了他一下]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="463.896" duration="0.864" /> **ERICA:** Hey what?
> 嘿，什么？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="464.76" duration="9.792" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** <Note>That's it</Note>, just hey. Like at the end of a dance, HEY! [she starts nibbling his hand] Hey. He-hey.
> 就这样，嘿。就像在舞会结束时，嘿！[她开始咬他的手]嘿。他嘿。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语">

- **That's it**：惯用语"就是这样/对了"（≠ that's all）（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **Like at the end of a dance**：like 作介词"像……一样"，后接名词短语（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Library. Phoebe is singing.*
> 🎬 *图书馆。菲比在唱歌。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：使役动词 / 定语从句 / 惯用句式 / 反讽双重否定

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="474.552" duration="2.544" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** [singing] <Note>There'll be times when you get older</Note>
> （唱歌）有时候你会变老
<GrammarTip points="存在句,关系从句,将来时">

- **There'll be = There will be**：There be 存在句的**未来式**（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **times when you get older**：**when 引导定语从句**（台：关系从句）修饰 times——"你会变老的那些时刻"；when 是关系副词 = at which（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

when you'll want to sleep with people
> 当你想和别人睡觉的时候

just <Note>to make them like you</Note>. . .
> 只是为了让他们喜欢你。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,使役动词">

- **to make them like you**：**不定式**（台：不定词）作目的状语"只为了……"；**make + 宾语 + 原形 like**——使役动词句型 S+V+O+C"让他们喜欢你"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

But don't.
> 但是不要。
<GrammarTip points="祈使句,省略">

- **But don't (do that)**：祈使句否定式，do 后宾语因承接上文省略（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Cause <Note>that's another thing that you don't wanna do</Note>, everybody
> 因为这是另一件你不想做的事，大家
<GrammarTip points="关系从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">that</span> <span class="v">'s</span> <span class="c">another thing (that) you don't wanna do</span></p>

- **another thing that you don't wanna do**：**that 引导定语从句**（台：关系从句）修饰 thing；that 在从句中作 do 的宾语，可省略；wanna = want to（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

That's another thing that you don't wanna do.
> 那是你不想做的另一件事。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="477.096" duration="1.704" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Excellent!
> 太好了！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="478.8" duration="2.208" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Very informative!
> 信息量很大！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="481.008" duration="2.352" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** <Note>Not at all inappropriate</Note>!
> 一点也不不不合适！
<GrammarTip points="双重否定,反讽">

- **Not at all inappropriate**：not at all"完全不" + inappropriate"不妥"——双重否定字面上="非常妥当"，实际是三人组的**反讽**：这歌给小孩听太不妥了（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="483.36" duration="3.792" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** <Note>Thank you for coming</Note> everybody. <Note>There're cookies in the back</Note>.
> 谢谢大家的光临。后面有饼干。
<GrammarTip points="动名词,存在句">

- **Thank you for + 动名词 coming**：for 是介词，后接 V-ing——"谢谢光临"（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **There're cookies in the back**：There be 存在句，**主谓一致**（台：主语动词一致性）——复数 cookies 配 are（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="487.152" duration="2.04" /> **ROB:** That was great, the kids loved you.
> 太好了，孩子们都爱你。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="489.192" duration="1.128" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Yay, I rock.
> 是啊，我喜欢摇滚。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="490.32" duration="6.768" /> **ROB:** And you know why? <Note>Because you told the truth, and nobody ever tells kids the truth</Note>.You were incredible.
> 你知道为什么吗？因为你说的是实话，没有人告诉孩子们真相。你太不可思议了。
<GrammarTip points="主谓一致,双宾语">

- **you know why?**：省略句 = Do you know why (they loved you)?（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- **nobody ever tells kids the truth**：**nobody 作主语，动词用单数** tells；tell + 双宾语 tell sb sth（[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="497.088" duration="0.696" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** But.
> 但是。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="497.784" duration="1.8" /> **ROB:** <Note>How did you know there was a but</Note>?
> 你怎么知道有一个但是？
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,名词化">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">did know</span> <span class="o">(that) there was a but</span></p>

- **How did you know (that) there was a but?**：know 后接宾语从句（台：名词从句），主句倒装 did you know、从句不倒装；**a but**——连词 but 名词化"一个'但是'"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="499.584" duration="3.432" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** I sense these things. <Note>It was either but or butter</Note>.
> 我能感觉到这些东西。要么是黄油，要么是黄油。
<GrammarTip points="对等连接词">

- **either A or B**："不是 A 就是 B"——but / butter 谐音梗（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="503.016" duration="6.048" /> **ROB:** The thing is, I think some of the parents, <Note>they were kinda hopin' that you'd play more songs</Note> about like, barnyard animals.
> 问题是，我想有些家长，他们有点希望你能多放一些关于谷仓里的动物的歌。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,情态动词">

- **The thing is (that)...**：惯用句式"问题是……"，is 后接表语从句（台：名词从句）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **they were kinda hopin' (that) you'd play...**：**'d = would**——过去进行式 was hopin' + would 构成**过去将来**的委婉请求"希望您能唱……"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="509.064" duration="1.248" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** I can do that.
> 我能做到。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="510.312" duration="0.768" /> **ROB:** Really?
> 真的吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="511.08" duration="0.6" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Yeah.
> 是的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="511.68" duration="3.552" /> **ROB:** Because <Note>that would be fantastic</Note>. What? You wanna kiss me?
> 因为那太棒了。什么？你想吻我吗？
<GrammarTip points="情态动词">

- **that would be fantastic**：**would** 表假设"（你要真唱）那就太棒了"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="515.232" duration="1.368" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** <Note>Thinkin' about it</Note>.
> 想想看。
<GrammarTip points="省略,动词时态">

- **(I'm) thinkin' about it**：口语省略主语与 be 动词的**现在进行式**——"（我）正琢磨呢"；以彼之道还施彼身，回敬上集 Rob 的同款台词（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *San Diego Zoo. Ross and the janitor are in the nocturnal house.*
> 🎬 *圣地亚哥动物园。罗斯和看门人在夜间活动的房子里。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：同位语排比 / 被动语态 / 定语从句省略 / Do you have any idea 句型

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="516.6" duration="8.424" /> **JANITOR:** Ahh, the bat. <Note>Ambassador of darkness</Note>, <Note>flitting out of his cave like a winged messenger</Note>, sightless spectre of the <Note>macabe</Note>.
> 啊，蝙蝠。黑暗的使者，像一个长着翅膀的信使一样从洞穴里飞出来，是一个看不见的麦克白幽灵。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,同位语,分词">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="516.6" duration="8.424" />

**原句**：The bat. Ambassador of darkness, flitting out of his cave like a winged messenger, sightless spectre of the macabe.

**层次划分**：

- 三个名词词组作 **the bat 的同位语**排比：Ambassador of darkness（黑暗大使）/ a winged messenger（长翅膀的信使）/ sightless spectre（盲视的幽灵）——清洁工一开口就是恐怖片腔（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **flitting out of his cave like a winged messenger**：**现在分词短语作后置修饰**——"扑棱着飞出洞穴"；like + 名词"像……"（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）
- **原文勘误**：`macabe` 应为 **macabre**"可怖的"（法语借词，词尾 -bre）

**翻译**：蝙蝠啊——黑暗的大使，如插翅信使般掠出洞穴，是那看不见的可怖幽灵。

**语法要点**：同位语排比（Ch.2）；分词短语后置（Ch.16）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="525.024" duration="2.256" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Buddy, my monkey?
> 伙计，我的猴子？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="527.28" duration="5.472" /> **JANITOR:** Oh, yeah, right. <Note>There was a break-in</Note>, few months back, inside job. <Note>Your monkey was taken</Note>.
> 哦，是的，对。几个月前，有一次非法闯入，内部工作。你的猴子被抓了。
<GrammarTip points="存在句,被动语态">

- **There was a break-in**：There be 存在句**过去式**——"发生过一起闯入"；break-in 是复合名词（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **Your monkey was taken**：**过去式被动**"被人偷走了"；inside job"内鬼干的"（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="532.752" duration="5.136" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Oh my God. But <Note>the zoo told me that my monkey was dead</Note>.
> 哦，我的天啊。但是动物园告诉我我的猴子死了。
<GrammarTip points="双宾语,名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">the zoo</span> <span class="v">told</span> <span class="o">me</span> <span class="o">that my monkey was dead</span></p>

- **tell sb + that 从句**：tell 接双宾语——间接宾语 me + 直接宾语（that 引导的**宾语从句**，台：名词从句）；从句中 dead 是主语补语（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="537.888" duration="3.72" /> **JANITOR:** The zoo! <Note>Do you believe everything the zoo tells ya</Note>?
> 动物园！你相信动物园告诉你的一切吗？
<GrammarTip points="关系从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">do believe</span> <span class="o">everything (that) the zoo tells ya</span></p>

- **everything (that) the zoo tells ya**：**定语从句**（台：关系从句）修饰 everything；关系代词 that 作 tells 的宾语故省略——先行词是 everything 时**只能用 that**（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="541.608" duration="3.816" /> **ROSS GELLER:** That, <Note>that's the only thing the zoo's ever told me</Note>.
> 那是动物园唯一告诉我的事。
<GrammarTip points="关系从句,动词时态">

- **the only thing (that) the zoo's ever told me**：定语从句修饰 thing，that 作 told 的直接宾语故省略；**zoo's = zoo has**，**现在完成式 + ever**"至今（唯一）告诉过我的"（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="545.424" duration="8.784" /> **JANITOR:** Of course <Note>they're gonna say he's dead</Note>. They don't want the bad publicity. It's all a great big cover-up. <Note>Do you have any idea how high up this thing goes</Note>?
> 他们当然会说他死了。他们不想受到不好的宣传。这都是一个巨大的掩饰。你知道这东西有多高吗？
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,名词从句">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="545.424" duration="8.784" />

**原句**：They're gonna say he's dead. Do you have any idea how high up this thing goes?

**层次划分**：

- **they're gonna say (that) he's dead**：gonna = going to 表"（预料中）会"；say 后接省略 that 的宾语从句（台：名词从句）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **Do you have any idea how high up this thing goes?**：**Do you have any idea + 名词从句**"你知不知道……"；**how high up this thing goes** 整段作 idea 的同位语从句——how high (up) 引导、从句内**陈述语序不倒装**；goes 指"（阴谋）蔓延到多高层"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

**翻译**：他们当然会说他死了，就是怕负面新闻。这全是个天大的掩盖。你知不知道这事儿的来头有多大？

**语法要点**：gonna + 宾语从句（Ch.3/12）；idea + how 同位语从句（Ch.12）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="554.208" duration="1.992" /> **ROSS GELLER:** That guy Lipson?
> 那个叫李普森的家伙？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="556.2" duration="3.432" /> **JANITOR:** Lipson knows. <Note>Do you have any idea who else knows</Note>?
> 利普森知道。你知道还有谁知道吗？
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

- **who else knows**：**who else + 陈述语序**作 idea 的同位语从句——"还有谁知道内情"；who 在从句中作主语（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="559.632" duration="3.408" /> **ROSS GELLER:** No, I, I only know Lipson.
> 不，我，我只知道利普森。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="563.04" duration="9.144" /> **JANITOR:** Hmm, Lipson knows huh? Ahh, hello Mr. Opossum, <Note>enigma of the trees</Note>, <Note>upside-down denizen of the night</Note>, <Note>taunting gravity with</Note>...
> 嗯，利普森知道，嗯？啊，你好，负鼠先生，树木之谜，倒转的夜晚居民，用…嘲弄地心引力。。。
<GrammarTip points="同位语,复合形容词,分词">

- **enigma of the trees / upside-down denizen of the night**：又是同位语排比——"林间之谜""倒挂的暗夜居民"；**upside-down** 复合形容词（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)/[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- **taunting gravity with...**：**现在分词短语**收尾——"以……嘲弄重力"，话没说完就被 Ross 打断（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="572.184" duration="3.192" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Buddy, my monkey, my monkey.
> 伙计，我的猴子，我的猴子。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="575.376" duration="5.736" /> **JANITOR:** <Note>Word on the street</Note> - well, <Note>when I say street</Note>, I mean those little pretend streets they have here at the zoo.
> 街上的字——嗯，当我说街的时候，我指的是动物园里的那些小街。
<GrammarTip points="习语,副词从句,关系从句">

- **Word on the street (is that...)**：惯用语"坊间传闻……"——word 前省略 the、is 后省略 that（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **when I say street**：when 引导时间**状语从句**（台：副词从句）"当我说'街'的时候"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **those little pretend streets (that) they have here at the zoo**：定语从句省略 that（作 have 的宾语）（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="581.112" duration="1.728" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Of course.
> 当然。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="582.84" duration="4.656" /> **JANITOR:** Your monkey found a new career, in the entertainment field. <Note>That's all I know</Note>.
> 你的猴子在娱乐圈找到了新的职业。我只知道这些。
<GrammarTip points="关系从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">That</span> <span class="v">'s</span> <span class="c">all (that) I know</span></p>

- **all (that) I know**：all + 定语从句，关系代词 that 作 know 的宾语故省略——"我所知道的全部"（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="587.496" duration="2.256" /> **ROSS GELLER:** This is unbelievable.
> 真是难以置信。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="589.752" duration="3.192" /> **JANITOR:** So, <Note>what is this information worth to you</Note>, my friend?
> 那么，这个信息对你有什么价值，我的朋友？
<GrammarTip points="形容词,疑问句">

- **be worth + 名词**："值……"，worth 作补语；to you"对你来说"（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- 疑问词 what 提前、主谓倒装 is this information worth（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="592.944" duration="2.568" /> **ROSS GELLER:** <Note>Are you trying to get me to bribe you</Note>?
> 你是想让我贿赂你吗？
<GrammarTip points="不定词,使役">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">are trying</span> <span class="o">to get me to bribe you</span></p>

- **try to do**："试图……"，不定式（台：不定词）作宾语（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **get + 宾语 + to do**："让/说服某人做……"——get me to bribe you"让我贿赂你"（对比 make me bribe you 不加 to）（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="595.512" duration="0.864" /> **JANITOR:** Maybe.
> 也许吧。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="596.376" duration="2.856" /> **ROSS GELLER:** But <Note>you already told me everything</Note>.
> 但你已经把一切都告诉我了。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

- **you already told me everything**：already 常与**完成式**搭配（you've already told me），口语也用**过去式**——"你都已经说完了"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Library. Ross show up with a MonkeyShine Beer poster.*
> 🎬 *图书馆。罗斯拿着一张MonkeyShine啤酒海报出现。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：be going to / try to do / what 名词从句 / how 表语从句

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="599.232" duration="3.864" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Check it out, he actually is the MonkeyShine monkey.
> 你看，他实际上是一只猴子。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="603.096" duration="2.664" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Well, so <Note>what're you gonna do</Note>?
> 那你打算怎么办？
<GrammarTip points="将来时,疑问句">

- **What're you gonna do?**：= What are you going to do?——be going to 表"打算"；疑问词提前倒装（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="605.76" duration="4.92" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Well, <Note>I guess I'm gonna call the beer company and try to find out where he is</Note>.
> 好吧，我想我会打电话给啤酒公司，想知道他在哪里。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,不定词">

- **I guess (that)...**：guess 后省略 that 的宾语从句（台：名词从句）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **try to find out where he is**：try + 不定式"设法"；**where he is** 作 find out 的宾语从句——**陈述语序**（× where is he）（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="610.68" duration="3.528" /> **CHANDLER BING:** <Note>That's what I did when I lost my Clydesdales</Note>.
> 我丢了我的克莱兹代尔斯就是这么做的。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,副词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">That</span> <span class="v">'s</span> <span class="c">what I did when I lost my Clydesdales</span></p>

- **what I did**：what 引导**表语从句**（台：名词从句）"我（当初）就是这么干的"；what = the thing that（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **when I lost my Clydesdales**：when 时间**状语从句**（台：副词从句）；Clydesdales 是百威啤酒的招牌大马——Chandler 又在胡扯（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="614.208" duration="1.32" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** OK, hi again.
> 好的，又来了。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="615.528" duration="1.248" /> **ALL:** Hi Phoebe.
> 大家好，菲比。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="616.776" duration="3.528" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Today <Note>we're gonna start with some songs about barnyard animals</Note>.
> 今天我们将从一些关于谷仓动物的歌曲开始。
<GrammarTip points="将来时,短语动词">

- **start with + 名词**："以……开场"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **gonna start**：be going to 表计划（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[singing] Oh, the cow in the meadow goes moo,
> [唱]哦，草地上的牛咕咕叫，
<GrammarTip points="动词,拟声">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">the cow in the meadow</span> <span class="v">goes</span> <span class="o">moo</span></p>

- **go + 拟声词**："发出……叫声"——goes moo"哞哞叫"；in the meadow 介词短语后置定语（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Oh, the cow in the meadow goes moo.
> 哦，草地上的奶牛咕咕叫。

Then the farmer <Note>hits him on the head</Note> and grinds him up,
> 然后农夫打了他的头，把他碾碎，
<GrammarTip points="介词,短语动词">

- **hits him on the head**："打在他的头上"——身体部位前用 **the** 而非 his（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **grinds him up**：短语动词 grind up"碾碎"，代词 him 夹中间（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

And <Note>that's how we get hamburgers</Note>.
> 这就是我们吃汉堡包的方式。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">that</span> <span class="v">'s</span> <span class="c">how we get hamburgers</span></p>

- **how we get hamburgers**：how 引导**表语从句**（台：名词从句）——"汉堡就是这么来的"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

Nooowww, chickens!
> 不，鸡！

---

🎬 *Chandler and Joey's apartment. Monica, Rachel, and the guys are watching Days of Our Lives.*
> 🎬 *钱德勒和乔伊的公寓。莫妮卡、瑞秋和其他人在看我们的生活*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：定语从句 / 否定前移 / should have + 过去分词 / 感官动词

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="620.304" duration="2.28" /> **TV DOCTOR:** You're <Note>the only one who can save her</Note> Drake.
> 你是唯一能救她的德雷克的人。
<GrammarTip points="关系从句">

- **the only one who can save her**：**who 引导定语从句**（台：关系从句）修饰 one，who 作主语不可省；先行词被 the only 修饰时关系代词常用 that（the only one **that** can...）（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="622.584" duration="2.4" /> **JOEY ON TV:** Damnit, I'm a doctor, I'm not God.
> 该死，我是医生，我不是上帝。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="624.984" duration="3.264" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Well, <Note>there goes my whole belief system</Note>.
> 嗯，我的整个信仰体系都消失了。
<GrammarTip points="倒装">

<p class="svoc"><span class="v">There goes</span> <span class="s">my whole belief system</span></p>

- **there goes + 名词**：There 置句首、**全部倒装**——"（我的整个信仰体系）就这么崩塌了"；惯用句型 there goes...表"……泡汤了"（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[knock at the door]
> [敲门]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="628.248" duration="1.224" /> **ERICA:** It's Erica.
> 是艾丽卡。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="629.472" duration="3.048" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Oh my God, quick <Note>turn off the TV</Note>.
> 我的天啊，快把电视关掉。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词">

- **turn off the TV / turn the TV off**：短语动词 turn off"关掉"；宾语是名词可前可后，**代词必须夹中间**（turn it off）（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="632.52" duration="3.912" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** No no no, wait, <Note>I wanna see what happens</Note>.
> 不，等等，我想看看会发生什么。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">wanna see</span> <span class="o">what happens</span></p>

- **what happens**：what 引导**宾语从句**（台：名词从句），what 在从句中作主语；从句用**现在式代未来**——"接下来会发生什么"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="636.432" duration="3.888" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Uh, <Note>I get Leslie out of the coma</Note> and then <Note>we make out</Note>.
> 呃，我把莱斯利从昏迷中救出来，然后我们亲热。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词,动词时态">

- **get Leslie out of the coma**：get + 宾语 + 介词短语"把她从昏迷中弄醒"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **make out**：短语动词"亲热、拥吻"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- 剧透剧情用**现在式** get/make——历史现在式（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="640.32" duration="3.6" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Well <Note>how can that be</Note>, <Note>you were just kissing Sabrina</Note>?
> 那怎么可能，你刚才在吻萨布丽娜？
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,动词时态">

- **How can that be?**：惯用语"这怎么可能？"——that 指整件事（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **you were just kissing Sabrina**：**过去进行式 + just**"你刚才还在亲 Sabrina（怎么又跟 Leslie 好上了）"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="643.92" duration="3.84" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Rachel, <Note>it's a world where Joey is a neuro-surgeon</Note>.
> 瑞秋，乔伊是个神经外科医生。
<GrammarTip points="关系从句">

- **a world where Joey is a neuro-surgeon**：**where 引导定语从句**（台：关系从句）修饰 world——关系副词 where = in which；Monica 提醒：这剧里 Joey 都能当神经外科医生，还有什么不可能（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="647.76" duration="2.52" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Hey Erica, c'mon in.
> 嘿，艾丽卡，进来吧。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="650.28" duration="3.264" /> **ERICA:** How did you get here so fast, <Note>I just saw you in Salem</Note>?
> 你怎么来得这么快，我刚在塞勒姆看见你？
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

- **I just saw you in Salem**：**just + 过去式**"刚刚（还）……"——口语中 just 可配过去式（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="653.544" duration="4.536" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Right, they uh, <Note>they choppered me in</Note>. What's up?
> 对，他们，呃，他们把我砍了进去。怎么了？
<GrammarTip points="名词动用,被动">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">they</span> <span class="v">choppered</span> <span class="o">me</span> <span class="a">in</span></p>

- **choppered me in**：**名词 chopper（直升机）动用**——"用直升机把我送进来的"；临场编谎的神来一笔（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="658.08" duration="3.936" /> **ERICA:** Ohh, and I see you're having a little party too. Is she here, huh, huh?
> 哦，我看到你也在开一个小派对。她在这儿吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="662.016" duration="1.464" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Who?
> 谁？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="663.48" duration="5.304" /> **ERICA:** Sabrina. I know about you two. <Note>I saw you today kissing in the doctor's lounge</Note>.
> 萨布丽娜。我知道你们两个。我今天看见你在医生休息室接吻。
<GrammarTip points="感官动词,分词">
