<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">saw</span> <span class="o">you</span> <span class="c">kissing in the doctor's lounge</span></p>

- **see + 宾语 + 现在分词**：感官动词句型——"我亲眼看见你在医生休息室里亲嘴"；分词 kissing 作**宾语补足语**（台：补语）表"正在进行"（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="668.784" duration="2.904" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** <Note>It's not what you think</Note>, that was...
> 不是你想的那样，那是。。。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">It</span> <span class="v">'s not</span> <span class="c">what you think</span></p>

- **what you think**：what 引导**表语从句**（台：名词从句）——"事情不是你想的那回事"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="671.688" duration="4.776" /> **ERICA:** <Note>You told me I was the only one</Note>. [throws a glass of water in his face]
> 你告诉我只有我一个人。[往他脸上扔了一杯水]
<GrammarTip points="双宾语,名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">You</span> <span class="v">told</span> <span class="o">me</span> <span class="o">(that) I was the only one</span></p>

- **tell sb + that 从句**：双宾语结构；从句 was 与主句 told **时态呼应**（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="676.464" duration="17.112" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Alright look, that's it. <Note>I don't think we should see each other anymore</Note>, alright. Look, <Note>I know I should have told you this a long time ago</Note> but I am not Drake Remore, OK. I'm not even a doctor, I'm an actor. <Note>I just pretend to be a doctor</Note>.
> 好的，看，就是这样。我觉得我们不应该再见面了，好吗。听着，我知道我早就该告诉你了，但我不是德雷克·雷莫尔，好吗。我甚至不是医生，我是演员。我只是假装是个医生。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,否定前移,情态动词">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="676.464" duration="17.112" />

**原句**：I don't think we should see each other anymore. I know I should have told you this a long time ago but I am not Drake Remore. I just pretend to be a doctor.

**层次划分**：

- **I don't think we should see each other anymore**：**否定前移**——语义上是否定从句（I think we **shouldn't** see...），习惯移到主句；not...anymore"不再"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **I know (that) I should have told you this a long time ago**：**should have + 过去分词**"（早就）该告诉你（却没说）"——对过去的自责（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **I just pretend to be a doctor**：**pretend + 不定式**（台：不定词）"假装是……"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

**翻译**：我想我们不该再见面了。我知道我早就该告诉你：我不是德雷克·雷莫尔，我连医生都不是，我是个演员，只是演医生罢了。

**语法要点**：否定前移（Ch.12）；should have done（Ch.9）；pretend to do（Ch.4）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="693.576" duration="3.408" /> **ERICA:** Oh my God. Do the people at the hospital know about this?
> 哦，我的天啊。医院的人知道这件事吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="696.984" duration="2.784" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** <Note>Somebody wanna help me out here</Note>?
> 有人想帮我吗？
<GrammarTip points="省略,短语动词">

- **(Does) somebody wanna help me out here?**：口语省略助动词 does；wanna = want to；**help sb out**"帮某人解围"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="699.768" duration="6.336" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Oh, I know, I know. [Turns on the TV. Joey in on it.]
> 哦，我知道，我知道。[打开电视，乔伊在里面]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="706.104" duration="2.256" /> **ERICA:** How, <Note>how can you be here and there</Note>.
> 你怎么，怎么能在这里和那里。
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,对等连接词">

- **how can you be here and there**：疑问词 how + 倒装 can you be；here and there 并列——"你怎么能同时在这儿又在（电视里的）那儿"（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)/[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="708.36" duration="2.496" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** 'Cause it's a television show.
> 因为这是一个电视节目。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="710.856" duration="1.656" /> **ERICA:** Drake, <Note>what're you getting at</Note>?
> 德雷克，你在说什么？
<GrammarTip points="短语动词">

- **What're you getting at?**：**get at**"意指、暗示"——"你到底想说什么？"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="712.512" duration="1.992" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** I'm not Drake.
> 我不是德雷克。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="714.504" duration="4.944" /> **ROSS GELLER:** That's right, he's not Drake, <Note>he's Hans Remore, Drake's evil twin</Note>.
> 没错，他不是德雷克，他是汉斯·雷莫尔，德雷克的邪恶孪生兄弟。
<GrammarTip points="同位语">

- **Hans Remore, Drake's evil twin**：同位语——"德雷克的邪恶双胞胎"补充说明 Hans Remore；Ross 瞎编救场（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="719.448" duration="1.224" /> **ERICA:** Is this true?
> 这是真的吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="720.672" duration="10.008" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Yes, yes it is true. And I know this because, <Note>because he pretended to be Drake to, to sleep with me</Note>. [throws water in his face]
> 是的，是的，这是真的。我知道这是因为，因为他假装是德雷克·托，和我睡觉。[往他脸上泼水]
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,不定词">

- **because he pretended to be Drake**：because 引导原因**状语从句**（台：副词从句）；**pretend to be**"假装是"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **to sleep with me**：**不定式**（台：不定词）作目的状语——"为了骗我上床"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="730.68" duration="6.456" /> **MONICA GELLER:** And then <Note>he told me he would run away with me</Note>, and he didn't. [throws water in his face]
> 然后他告诉我他会和我私奔，但他没有。[往他脸上泼水]
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,动词时态">

- **he told me (that) he would run away with me**：tell 双宾 + 宾语从句；**would run** 是**过去将来**——从过去某时看"将会私奔"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **and he didn't (run away)**：省略重复部分（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="737.136" duration="6.168" /> **CHANDLER BING:** And <Note>you left the toilet seat up</Note>, you bastard. [throws water in his face]
> 你把马桶座忘在上面了，你这个混蛋。[往他脸上泼水]
<GrammarTip points="基本句型">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">left</span> <span class="o">the toilet seat</span> <span class="c">up</span></p>

- **leave + 宾语 + 补语**："把马桶圈撂在（掀起的）状态"——S+V+O+C，up 作**宾语补足语**（台：补语）；Chandler 纯粹凑热闹泼一杯（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="743.304" duration="1.32" /> **ERICA:** Is all this true?
> 这都是真的吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="744.624" duration="13.104" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Yes, I'm afraid it is. <Note>You deserve much better than me</Note> Erica. <Note>You deserve to be with the real Drake</Note>, <Note>he's the one you fell in love with</Note>. Go to Salem, find him, he's the guy for you.
> 恐怕是的。你应该得到比我更好的东西，艾丽卡。你应该和真正的德雷克在一起，他是你爱上的人。去塞勒姆，找到他，他是你的真命天子。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,情态动词,关系从句">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="744.624" duration="13.104" />

**原句**：You deserve much better than me. You deserve to be with the real Drake, he's the one you fell in love with.

**层次划分**：

- **You deserve much better than me**：deserve + 名词"配得上"；**much better than me** 比较级 + than 宾格（口语；正式 = than I）；better 后省略了名词（a better guy）（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- **You deserve to be with the real Drake**：**deserve + 不定式**（台：不定词）"理应得到……"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **he's the one (that) you fell in love with**：**定语从句**（台：关系从句）修饰 the one——关系代词 that 作介词 with 的宾语故省略；**fall in love with** 短语（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **Go to Salem, find him**：两个祈使句并列（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

**翻译**：艾丽卡，你配得上比我好得多的人。你应该跟真正的德雷克在一起——你爱上的本来就是他。去塞勒姆找他吧，他才是你的归宿。

**语法要点**：deserve + 名词/to do（Ch.4/7）；介词 + 关系代词省略（Ch.14）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="757.728" duration="2.112" /> **ERICA:** Oh Hans. [They kiss]
> 哦，汉斯。[他们接吻]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="759.84" duration="3.672" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Hans...Hans...Yo evil twin.
> 汉斯……汉斯……你们这邪恶的双胞胎。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="763.512" duration="6.192" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Right. Goodbye Erica, good luck in Salem. Take care
> 对。再见，艾丽卡，祝你在塞勒姆好运。当心

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="769.704" duration="4.152" /> **ERICA:** I'll never forget you Hans. [Joey shuts the door in her face]
> 我永远不会忘记你，汉斯。[乔伊当面把门关上]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="773.856" duration="3.624" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** OK, alright, <Note>the people who threw the water</Note>.
> 好吧，好吧，那些扔水的人。
<GrammarTip points="关系从句">

- **the people who threw the water**：**who 引导定语从句**（台：关系从句）修饰 people，who 作主语不可省——Joey 关上门就开始找泼水的人算账（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Central Perk. Phoebe and Rob are sitting on the couch.*
> 🎬 *中央公园。菲比和罗布坐在沙发上。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：现在完成式 / 虚拟建议 / want sb to do / 宾语从句

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="777.48" duration="1.656" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Fired! Why?
> 被解雇了！为什么？
<GrammarTip points="省略,被动语态">

- **Fired!**：省略句 = (I'm) fired!——**过去分词作补语**，get/be fired"被解雇"（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="779.136" duration="5.184" /> **ROB:** The library board <Note>has had a lot of complaints</Note> from parents about some of the stuff in your songs.
> 图书馆董事会收到了很多家长对你歌曲中某些内容的投诉。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,名词词组">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">The library board</span> <span class="v">has had</span> <span class="o">a lot of complaints</span></p>

- **has had**：**现在完成式**——"（至今）已收到大量投诉"，强调对现在的影响（你被开了）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **complaints from parents about some of the stuff**：complaint **from** sb **about** sth——两个介词各管各的（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="784.32" duration="4.728" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** I can't believe it. Did you tell your board about <Note>how kids want to hear the truth</Note>?
> 我真不敢相信。你有没有告诉董事会孩子们希望听到真相？
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

- **how kids want to hear the truth**：**how 引导名词从句**（台：名词从句）作介词 about 的宾语——"孩子们多想听真话这一点"；从句内陈述语序（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="789.048" duration="0.768" /> **ROB:** No.
> 没有。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="789.816" duration="0.984" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** I see.
> 我明白了。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="790.8" duration="2.736" /> **ROB:** Maybe <Note>if you just played some regular kiddie songs</Note>.
> 也许你只要演奏一些普通的童谣就行了。
<GrammarTip points="虚拟语气,副词从句">

- **if you just played...**：if 条件**状语从句**（台：副词从句）用**过去式 played**——与现在事实相反的委婉建议"你要是肯唱几首普通儿歌（就好了）"；主句 (it would be fine) 省略（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="793.536" duration="5.064" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** No. What do you, <Note>what do you want me to be</Note>, like some stupid, big, like, purple dinosaur?
> 不。你，你想让我变成什么样子，像一只愚蠢的，大的，像紫色的恐龙？
<GrammarTip points="不定词,介词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">do want</span> <span class="o">me</span> <span class="c">to be what</span></p>

- **want + 宾语 + 不定式补语**："你想让我当什么？"——what 提前倒装；**不定式**（台：不定词）to be 作宾语补足语（台：补语）（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **like some stupid, big, purple dinosaur**：like 介词"像……"；暗讽紫色恐龙 Barney（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="798.6" duration="1.92" /> **ROB:** <Note>I'm not saying you have to be Barney</Note>.
> 我不是说你一定要成为巴尼。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,动词时态">

- **I'm not saying (that) you have to be Barney**：**现在进行式 say** 此处表"我的意思并不是"；从句 have to be"必须成为"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="800.52" duration="1.224" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Who's Barney.
> 谁是巴尼。

---

🎬 *Central Perk. The whole gang is there.*
> 🎬 *中央公园。整个帮派都在那里。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：短语动词 / 现在完成式 / 双重所有格 / used to

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="801.744" duration="9.504" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Well, <Note>I tracked down Marcel</Note> and get this, he's healthy, he's happy, and <Note>he's right here in New York filming Outbreak II - The Virus Takes Manhattan</Note>.
> 嗯，我找到了马塞尔，他很健康，很快乐，他就在纽约拍摄《暴发II》——病毒占领了曼哈顿。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,短语动词,分词">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="801.744" duration="9.504" />

**原句**：I tracked down Marcel and get this, he's healthy, he's happy, and he's right here in New York filming Outbreak II.

**层次划分**：

- **I tracked down Marcel**：短语动词 **track down**"追踪到"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **get this**：惯用语"你听好了"——祈使句引起注意（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **he's healthy, he's happy, and he's right here in New York**：三个 S+V+C 排比（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）
- **filming Outbreak II - The Virus Takes Manhattan**：**现在分词短语作补充说明**——"（人就在纽约）拍着《爆发 II》"；The Virus Takes Manhattan 是电影副标题，整句 S+V+O（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）

**翻译**：我找到马塞尔的下落了——你听好：它身体健康、心情愉快，而且人就在纽约拍《爆发 II：病毒攻占曼哈顿》。

**语法要点**：track down（Ch.10）；分词短语补充（Ch.16）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="811.248" duration="1.704" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** You're kidding.
> 你在开玩笑吧。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

- **You're kidding**：**现在进行式**——"你在开玩笑吧！"kid 作动词"开玩笑"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="812.952" duration="2.016" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** This is amazing.
> 这太棒了。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="814.968" duration="1.584" /> **ROSS GELLER:** I know.
> 我知道。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="816.552" duration="4.152" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** <Note>I finally get a part on TV</Note> and <Note>the monkey's makin' movies</Note>.
> 我终于在电视上得到了一个角色，猴子也在拍电影。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,对比">

- **I finally get a part on TV**：**现在式**叙述刚刚到手的成就（≈ I've finally gotten）；part"角色"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **the monkey's makin' movies**：**现在进行式**"（人家）都在拍电影了"——makin' = making；两句 and 并列，人猴对比显失落（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="820.704" duration="1.728" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** OK, Rachel, I'm ready.
> 好了，瑞秋，我准备好了。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="822.432" duration="1.512" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** OK.
> 好的。

[little kid enters]
> [小孩进来]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="823.944" duration="4.464" /> **KID:** Excuse me. <Note>Is this where the singing lady is that tells the truth</Note>?
> 对不起。这就是那个说实话的女歌手吗？
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,关系从句">

- **Is this where the singing lady is...?**：**where 引导表语从句**（台：名词从句）——"那位唱歌的女士是在这儿吗？"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **the singing lady that tells the truth**：**that 引导定语从句**（台：关系从句）修饰 lady，that 作主语不可省（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="828.408" duration="2.112" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Um, yeah <Note>I guess that's me</Note>.
> 嗯，是的，我想是我。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

- **I guess (that) that's me**：guess 后省略 that 的宾语从句（台：名词从句）；that's me"（说的）是我"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="830.52" duration="2.328" /> **KID:** [shouting out the door] She's here.
> （在门外喊叫）她来了。

[a rush of kids enter]
> [一群孩子涌入]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="832.848" duration="2.832" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** [singing] Sometimes men love women,
> 有时候男人爱女人，

sometimes men love men,
> 有时候男人爱男人，

and then there are bisexuals,
> 还有双性恋者，

<Note>though some just say they're kidding themselves</Note>.
> 尽管有些人只是说他们在开玩笑。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,名词从句">

- **though some just say...**：**though 引导让步状语从句**（台：副词从句）"尽管有人说……"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **say (that) they're kidding themselves**：省略 that 的宾语从句；**kid oneself**"自欺欺人"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

la la-la-la la-la-la-la-la-la-la...
> 啦啦啦啦啦啦啦啦。。。

---

🎬 *City street. The whole gang is walking up to the movie set.*
> 🎬 *城市街。整个团伙正走向电影布景。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：完成式 + in 时段 / 被动 be allowed to / 双重所有格 / 倒装

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="835.68" duration="4.776" /> **ROSS GELLER:** This is so exciting, <Note>I haven't seen my monkey in almost a year</Note>.
> 这太令人兴奋了，我已经快一年没看到我的猴子了。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">haven't seen</span> <span class="o">my monkey</span> <span class="a">in almost a year</span></p>

- **haven't seen...in almost a year**：**现在完成式 + in + 时段**——"已经快一年没见了"；in + 时段表"距今多久以来"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="840.456" duration="8.976" /> **CHANDLER BING:** What, you never look down in the shower? Oh please. <Note>I'm not allowed to make one joke</Note> in the monkey-is-penis genre?
> 什么，你洗澡时从不低头？哦，求你了。我不允许在《猴子是阴茎类型》里开玩笑？
<GrammarTip points="被动语态,不定词">

- **I'm not allowed to make one joke**：**被动语态 be allowed + 不定式**（台：不定词）——"连一个这种段子都不让我讲？"；主动式 allow sb to do → 被动 sb be allowed to do，to 保留（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **in the ... genre**：in + 领域"在……这个门类里"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="849.432" duration="5.4" /> **SECURITY GUARD:** C'mon people, <Note>back up</Note> please, back up, c'mon, c'mon, c'mon, c'mon.
> 来吧，请退后，退后，来吧，来吧，来吧，来吧。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词">

- **back up**：短语动词"往后退"——祈使句省略主语（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="854.832" duration="4.44" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Uh, excuse me, uh, where can we find the monkey?
> 劳驾，我们在哪里能找到猴子？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="859.272" duration="2.064" /> **SECURITY GUARD:** I'm sorry guys, <Note>closed set</Note>.
> 对不起，伙计们，已经关门了。
<GrammarTip points="复合形容词,省略">

- **closed set**："封闭片场（闲人免进）"——过去分词 closed 作定语；整句省略 = It's a closed set（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="861.336" duration="8.568" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Uh, I'm sorry, you don't understand, I'm, I'm, <Note>I'm a friend of his</Note>. We uh, <Note>we used to live together</Note>.
> 呃，对不起，你不明白，我，我，我是他的朋友。我们，呃，我们曾经住在一起。
<GrammarTip points="双重所有格,惯用语">

- **a friend of his**：**双重所有格**——"他的朋友之一"（= one of his friends）；of + 名词性物主代词 his（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **we used to live together**：**used to + 原形**"过去曾经（现在不了）"；注意与 be used to V-ing"习惯于"区分（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="869.904" duration="3.864" /> **SECURITY GUARD:** Yeah, and <Note>I have a time share in the Pocanos with Flipper</Note>.
> 是的，我和Flipper在Pocanos有一段时间。
<GrammarTip points="名词词组,讽刺">

- **a time share in the Pocanos with Flipper**：time share"分时度假房"；with Flipper"跟海豚 Flipper 合住"——保安反讽：你跟猴子同居过？我还跟海豚有分时度假屋呢（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="873.768" duration="2.4" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Ross, <Note>there he is</Note>.
> 罗斯，他来了。
<GrammarTip points="倒装">

<p class="svoc"><span class="a">There</span> <span class="s">he</span> <span class="v">is</span></p>

- **there he is**：There 置句首——主语是**代词** he，主谓**不倒装**（对比 There goes my belief system）；表"（快看）他在那儿！"（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="876.168" duration="28.296" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Hey, hey buddy, Marcel. Marcel. [Marcel doesn't react so Ross starts singing] <Note>In the jungle, the mighty jungle the lion sleeps tonight</Note>. [no reaction from Marcel, Monica and Joey urge him on] In the jungle, the mighty jungle the lion sleeps tonight. [Marcel looks over and everyone joins in] a-weema-way, a-weema-way..... [Marcel runs over and hops up on Ross's shoulder]
> 嘿，嘿，伙计，马塞尔。马塞尔。[马赛尔没有反应，罗斯开始唱歌]在丛林中，狮子今晚睡在壮丽的丛林中。[马赛尔、莫妮卡和乔伊没有反应，催促他继续]在丛林中，狮子今晚睡在巨大的丛林中。[马塞尔看了看，每个人都加入进来]一周一周，一周一周。。。。。[马塞尔跑过去，跳到罗斯的肩膀上]
<GrammarTip points="名词词组,动词时态">

- **In the jungle, the mighty jungle, the lion sleeps tonight**：the mighty jungle 是 the jungle 的**同位语**（带形容词 mighty"强大的"）——重复呼唤式歌词；sleeps 现在式（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- 此曲为《The Lion Sleeps Tonight》，正是第一季 Marcel 最爱的歌

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *The next time at the movie set.*
> 🎬 *下一次在电影现场*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：so that 目的从句 / unless / refuse·convince + to do / used to

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="904.464" duration="2.184" /> **SECURITY GUARD:** Uh, excuse me folks this is a uhh...
> 呃，对不起，伙计们，这是一个。。。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="906.648" duration="6.768" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Closed set. We know but <Note>we're friends with the monkey</Note>. [guard lets them in]
> 闭集。我们知道，但我们是猴子的朋友。[警卫让他们进来]
<GrammarTip points="介词">

- **be friends with sb**："跟某人是朋友"，with 表"与……（的关系）"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="913.416" duration="11.232" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Good morning. Hey pal, <Note>look who I brought</Note>. It's your old friend Harry Elefante. [Marcel grabs the elephant doll and throws it to the ground]
> 早上好。嘿，伙计，看看我带了谁来。是你的老朋友哈里·埃莉芬特。[马赛尔抓起大象娃娃，把它扔到地上]
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,双关">

- **look who I brought**：who 引导**宾语从句**（台：名词从句）作 look 的宾语——"看我带谁来了"；从句内 who 是 brought 的宾语、语序不倒装（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **Harry Elefante**：谐音梗——Elefante 音近 elephant（大象）；老朋友指第一季 Marcel 的玩偶（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="924.648" duration="2.544" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Woah, dude, burn.
> 哇，伙计，烧了。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="927.192" duration="4.176" /> **ROSS GELLER:** <Note>I don't get it</Note>, <Note>he seemed so happy to see me yesterday</Note>.
> 我不明白，他昨天见到我似乎很高兴。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,系动词">

- **I don't get it**：惯用语"我不明白"——get = understand（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **he seemed (to be) so happy to see me yesterday**：seem 作**系动词**（台：连缀动词）+ 形容词 happy；**不定式**（台：不定词）to see me 作原因状语"见到我（很开心）"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="931.368" duration="5.208" /> **TRAINER:** Hey <Note>don't take it personal</Note>, <Note>he's under a lot of pressure</Note>, ya know, starring in a movie and all.
> 嘿，别把它当成私人的事，他承受着很大的压力，你知道，主演电影等等。
<GrammarTip points="介词,分词">

- **don't take it personal**：take + 宾语 + 补语"别往心里去"；**规范说法应为 personally**（副词作补语），口语常说 personal（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **under a lot of pressure**：under + 名词"承受着……"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **starring in a movie and all**：**现在分词短语**补充说明压力来源；and all"什么的、等等"（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="936.576" duration="2.952" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Now just <Note>how big of a star is Marcel</Note>?
> 马塞尔到底是个多大的明星？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,名词词组">

- **how big of a star is Marcel?**：**how big of a + 名词**"多大的……"；疑问句倒装 is Marcel——"马塞尔到底是多大牌的明星？"（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="939.528" duration="3.48" /> **TRAINER:** <Note>In human terms</Note>, <Note>I'd say</Note> Cybill Shepard.
> 用人类的话来说，我会说是西比尔·谢泼德。
<GrammarTip points="介词,情态动词">

- **in human terms**：惯用语"按人类的标准来说"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **I'd say...**：**'d = would**——"要我说的话……"，would 表委婉推断（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="943.008" duration="0.744" /> **ALL:** Woah.
> 哇。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="943.752" duration="7.368" /> **CHANDLER BING:** [to guys wering yellow isolation suits] So, <Note>are you guys in the movie, or are you just really paranoid</Note>.
> （对穿黄色隔离服的人说）你们是在电影里，还是真的很偏执。
<GrammarTip points="选择疑问句">

- **are you guys in the movie, or are you just really paranoid?**：**or 连接的选择疑问句**——两个完整疑问结构并列（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

DIRECTOR'S ASSISTANT: Hey Sal, <Note>Jerry wants to know if the monkey's ready for the subway set</Note>?
> 导演助理：嗨，萨尔，杰瑞想知道猴子是否准备好去地铁了？
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

- **wants to know if the monkey's ready**：want + 不定式 to know；**if（是否）引导宾语从句**（台：名词从句）——注意从句**陈述语序** the monkey's ready，不倒装（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="951.12" duration="5.352" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Uh, excuse me. Jerry is the director, <Note>which one's he</Note>?
> 呃，对不起。杰瑞是导演，他是哪一个？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句">

- **which one's he?**：= which one is he?——which + one 表"（范围内）哪一个"；口语倒装（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

DIRECTOR'S ASSISTANT: The one in the director's chair.
> 董事会主席的助理。
<GrammarTip points="介词">

- **The one in the director's chair**：**in + 名词**介词短语作 the one 的后置定语——"坐在导演椅上那位"；所有格 director's（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="956.472" duration="3.864" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Gotcha. Phoebs, walk with me.
> 明白了。菲比，跟我走。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="960.336" duration="3.552" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** OK. Um, <Note>how come I'm walking with you</Note>?
> 好的。我怎么能和你一起走？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句">

- **How come + 陈述语序**："为什么……？"——**How come I'm walking with you?**（× How come am I walking...）how come 后**不倒装**，这是它与 why 的最大区别（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="963.888" duration="10.248" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Well, we're, we're just goin' over here <Note>so that we can get away from the horrible flesh eating virus</Note>, <Note>for the love of God</Note> woman, listen to me. <Note>Is he lookin', is he lookin'</Note>?
> 好吧，我们，我们只是到这里来，这样我们就可以远离可怕的食肉病毒，看在上帝的份上，女人，听我说。他在看吗，他在看吗？
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,副词从句,惯用语">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="963.888" duration="10.248" />

**原句**：We're just goin' over here so that we can get away from the horrible flesh eating virus, for the love of God woman, listen to me. Is he lookin', is he lookin'?

**层次划分**：

- 主干：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">we</span> <span class="v">'re goin'</span> <span class="a">over here</span></p>
- **so that we can get away from the horrible flesh eating virus**：**so that 引导目的状语从句**（台：副词从句）"以便……"；get away from"躲开"；flesh eating 复合形容词"食肉的"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- **for the love of God**：感叹插入语"看在上帝的份上"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **Is he lookin'?**：**现在进行式**重复追问——Joey 假装躲避病毒，实际在偷瞄导演（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

**翻译**：我们、我们就是往这边走，好躲开那个可怕的食肉病毒——我的姑奶奶，听我说！他在看吗？他在看吗？

**语法要点**：so that 目的从句（Ch.13）；进行式追问（Ch.3）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="974.136" duration="1.128" /> **SUSIE:** We've got a problem.
> 我们有个问题。

DIRECTOR'S ASSISTANT: Tell me.
> 导演助理：告诉我。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="975.264" duration="5.088" /> **SUSIE:** I can't do Chris's makeup. <Note>She refuses to acknowledge that she has a moustasche</Note>.
> 我不能给克里斯化妆。她拒绝承认自己有一只老鼠。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,名词从句">

- **refuse + 不定式**："拒绝做……"——refuses to acknowledge（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **acknowledge (that) she has a moustache**：acknowledge 后接 that 宾语从句（台：名词从句）；**原文勘误**：`moustasche` 应为 **moustache**（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

DIRECTOR'S ASSISTANT: Is it bad?
> 导演助理：不好吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="980.352" duration="9.072" /> **SUSIE:** <Note>It looks like one of her eyebrows fell down</Note>. Now <Note>unless someone convinces her to let me bleach it</Note>, Jean-Claude Van Damme <Note>is gonna be making out with Gabe Kaplan</Note>.
> 看起来她的一条眉毛掉了下来。除非有人说服她让我漂白，否则让-克劳德·范达姆会和盖布·卡普兰亲热。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,副词从句,动词时态">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="980.352" duration="9.072" />

**原句**：It looks like one of her eyebrows fell down. Unless someone convinces her to let me bleach it, Jean-Claude Van Damme is gonna be making out with Gabe Kaplan.

**层次划分**：

- **It looks like one of her eyebrows fell down**：**look like + 从句**"看起来好像……"；**one of + 复数名词** eyebrows"其中一条眉毛"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **unless someone convinces her to let me bleach it**：**unless = if...not** 引导条件**状语从句**（台：副词从句），**现在式代未来**；**convince sb to do**"说服某人做"；let + 宾语 + 原形 bleach（使役动词）（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **is gonna be making out with Gabe Kaplan**：**be going to + 进行式**——"（那画面就会变成）范·达美要亲的是盖布·卡普兰"；未来进行式想象即将上演的画面（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

**翻译**：看着就像她一条眉毛掉到了上唇。除非有人劝她让我把这撮小胡子漂白了，否则范·达美待会儿亲热的就是盖布·卡普兰了。

**语法要点**：unless + 现在式（Ch.13）；convince sb to do（Ch.4）；未来进行式（Ch.3）

</GrammarTip>

DIRECTOR'S ASSISTANT: I'll talk to her.
> 导演助理：我会和她谈谈。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="989.424" duration="1.392" /> **SUSIE:** I hate actors.
> 我讨厌演员。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="990.816" duration="6.432" /> **CHANDLER BING:** [to an extra in fatigues] Nice <Note>camoflauge</Note> man, <Note>for a minute there I almost didn't see ya</Note>.
> （对一个穿着睡衣的人说）漂亮的迷彩人，有一分钟我几乎没看见你。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

- **for a minute there I almost didn't see ya**：**过去式 didn't see**——"刚才那一下我差点没瞅见你"；for a minute there"刚才那阵儿"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **原文勘误**：`camoflauge` 应为 **camouflage**"迷彩"（字母 u/a 颠倒）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="997.248" duration="1.152" /> **SUSIE:** Excuse me.
> 对不起。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="998.4" duration="1.368" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Ahhhh.
> 啊。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="999.768" duration="2.04" /> **SUSIE:** Uh, is your name Chandler?
> 嗯，你叫钱德勒吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1001.808" duration="3.096" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Uh, yes, yes it is.
> 嗯，是的，是的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1004.904" duration="0.984" /> **SUSIE:** Chandler Bing?
> 钱德勒·宾？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1005.888" duration="3.6" /> **CHANDLER BING:** <Note>Do you know me or are you just really good at this game</Note>?
> 你认识我，还是你真的很擅长这个游戏？
<GrammarTip points="选择疑问句,介词">

- **Do you know me or are you just really good at this game?**：or 连接的选择疑问句（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）
- **be good at + 名词**："擅长……"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1009.488" duration="6.648" /> **SUSIE:** I'm Susie Moss. Fourth grade, glasses, <Note>I used to carry around a box of animal crackers like a purse</Note>.
> 我是苏西·莫斯。四年级的时候，戴着眼镜，我经常随身携带一盒动物饼干，就像钱包一样。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,短语动词,介词">

- **used to + 原形**："过去常常（现在不了）"——used to carry around（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **carry around**：短语动词"随身携带"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **like a purse**：like 介词"像个手提包似的"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1016.136" duration="5.544" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Susie Moss, right, yeah, wow, you look. . . <Note>great job growing up</Note>.
> 苏西·莫斯，对，是的，哇，你看起来。在成长过程中做得很好。
<GrammarTip points="动名词,惯用语">

- **great job (in) growing up**：**Great job (doing sth)!**"……干得漂亮！"——job 后接动名词；Chandler 词穷地夸"你长大这事儿干得真漂亮"（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1021.68" duration="4.776" /> **SUSIE:** <Note>It's nice to see you're not still wearing that denim cap</Note> with all the little mirrors on it.
> 很高兴看到你不再戴那顶有小镜子的牛仔帽了。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,名词从句,动词时态">

- **It's nice to see (that)...**：**不定式**（台：不定词）to see 作真正主语 it 的说明——"看到……真好"；see 后接省略 that 的宾语从句（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **you're not still wearing**：**现在进行式 + still**"没再一直戴着"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **with all the little mirrors on it**：**with + 名词 + 副词**独立结构作 cap 的定语——"上面缀满小镜子的"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1026.456" duration="5.736" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Oh, right, well yeah, <Note>I graduated fourth grade and realized I wasn't a pimp</Note>.
> 哦，对了，嗯，是的，我从四年级毕业，意识到我不是皮条客。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,对等连接词">

- **graduated fourth grade and realized (that) I wasn't a pimp**：and 连接两个并列谓语；realize 后接省略 that 的宾语从句（台：名词从句）（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1032.192" duration="6.0" /> **SUSIE:** Remember the class play? You, <Note>you pulled up my skirt</Note> and the entire auditorium saw my underpants.
> 还记得那场班级戏剧吗？你，你拉起我的裙子，整个礼堂都看到了我的内裤。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词">

- **pulled up my skirt**：短语动词 **pull up**"撩起、拉起"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **the entire auditorium saw my underpants**：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">the entire auditorium</span> <span class="v">saw</span> <span class="o">my underpants</span></p> entire"整个的"（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1038.192" duration="8.184" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Yes, back then I, uh, <Note>used humor as a defense mechanism</Note>. Thank God I don't do that anymore.
> 是的，那时候我，嗯，用幽默作为一种防御机制。谢天谢地，我不再那样做了。
<GrammarTip points="介词,惯用语">

- **use A as B**："把 A 当作 B 来用"——as 介词"作为"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **back then**："那时候"（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **Thank God (that) I don't do that anymore**：Thank God + 从句"谢天谢地……"；not...anymore"不再"（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

[cut to Monica and Rachel walking through the set]
> [切换到莫妮卡和瑞秋在布景中穿行]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1046.376" duration="3.432" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Oh my God, oh my God, oh my God.
> 哦，我的天，哦，我的天，哦，我的天。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1049.808" duration="2.016" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** What what what what?
> 什么？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1051.824" duration="5.952" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Jean-Claude Van Damme. <Note>I didn't know he was in this movie</Note>, he is so hot.
> 让·克劳德·范达姆。我不知道他在这部电影里，他太性感了。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

- **I didn't know (that) he was in this movie**：know 后省略 that 的宾语从句（台：名词从句）；主从句时态呼应 didn't know...was（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1057.776" duration="1.68" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Ya think?
> 你觉得呢？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1059.456" duration="5.064" /> **MONICA GELLER:** <Note>The muscles from Brussels</Note>, wham bam Van Damme, did you see Time Cop?
> 来自布鲁塞尔的肌肉，砰砰，范·达姆，你看到《时代警察》了吗？
<GrammarTip points="同位语">

- **The muscles from Brussels**：绰号式同位语——"布鲁塞尔肌肉"，Brussels 与 muscles 押头韵（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1064.52" duration="2.664" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** No, <Note>was he any good in it</Note>?
> 不，他做得好吗？
<GrammarTip points="惯用语">

- **be any good (in...)**："（在……里）表现出色吗？"——any 用于疑问句加强"究竟、到底"（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1067.184" duration="3.6" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Rachel, he like, totally changed time.
> 瑞秋，他喜欢，完全改变了时间。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1070.784" duration="3.096" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Wow, so <Note>why don't you go talk to him</Note>?
> 哇，那你为什么不去和他谈谈呢？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,建议">

- **Why don't you + 原形**：建议句型"你何不……？"——比直接命令委婉（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1073.88" duration="1.848" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Oh, yeah.
> 哦，是的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1075.728" duration="5.976" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** What, so you go over there, you tell him you think he's cute, <Note>what's the worst that could happen</Note>?
> 什么，你去那边，告诉他你觉得他很可爱，最糟糕的情况是什么？
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,关系从句,祈使句">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1075.728" duration="5.976" />

**原句**：You go over there, you tell him you think he's cute, what's the worst that could happen?

**层次划分**：

- **you go over there, you tell him...**：两个**现在式**分句排开，像列行动清单——口语"你去那儿，你跟他说……"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）
- **you think (that) he's cute**：think 后省略 that 的宾语从句（台：名词从句）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **what's the worst (that) could happen?**：**the worst** 后接定语从句（台：关系从句），关系代词 that 作主语——"最坏又能怎样？"；**could** 表可能性（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

**翻译**：你走过去，告诉他你觉得他帅——最坏还能坏到哪去？

**语法要点**：the worst that could happen（Ch.14）；宾语从句省略 that（Ch.12）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1081.704" duration="1.872" /> **MONICA GELLER:** He could hear me.
> 他能听到我说话。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1083.576" duration="2.616" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** OK, I'm doin' it for ya.
> 好吧，我是为你做的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1086.192" duration="5.784" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Oh Rachel don't, <Note>don't you dare</Note>, don't, don't. Tell him I cook.
> 哦，瑞秋，别，你敢，别，别。告诉他我做饭。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语">

- **Don't you dare**：惯用语"你敢！"——否定疑问形式的强烈警告（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **Tell him (that) I cook**：tell + 双宾 + 宾语从句——Monica 情急之下只憋出一句"跟他说我会做饭"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1091.976" duration="3.144" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Excuse me. Hi.
> 对不起。你好

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1095.12" duration="0.768" /> **VAN DAMME:** Hi.
> 嗨。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1095.888" duration="7.704" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Um, <Note>this is gonna sound kinda goofy</Note> but uhhm, my friend over there, <Note>who cooks by the way</Note>, um, she thinks you're cute.
> 嗯，这听起来有点傻，但是嗯，我那边的朋友，顺便说一句，她觉得你很可爱。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,关系从句,名词从句">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1095.888" duration="7.704" />

**原句**：This is gonna sound kinda goofy but my friend over there, who cooks by the way, she thinks you're cute.

**层次划分**：

- **this is gonna sound kinda goofy**：**be going to + 系动词 sound**"这话听起来会有点蠢"；kinda = kind of（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- 主干：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">my friend over there, who cooks by the way,</span> <span class="v">thinks</span> <span class="o">(that) you're cute</span></p> she 是 my friend 的重复指代（口语补述）
- **who cooks by the way**：**who 引导非限定性定语从句**（台：关系从句），前后逗号隔开、补充说明"（顺便说，她会做饭）"——Monica 的叮嘱被 Rachel 插进去了（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）
- **thinks (that) you're cute**：think + 宾语从句（台：名词从句）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

**翻译**：呃，这话听着有点冒昧：我那边那位朋友——对了，她会做饭哦——她觉得你很帅。

**语法要点**：非限定性关系从句打逗号（Ch.14）；插入语 by the way（Ch.8）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1103.592" duration="1.248" /> **VAN DAMME:** <Note>You don't think I'm cute</Note>?
> 你不觉得我可爱吗？
<GrammarTip points="否定疑问句">

- **You don't think I'm cute?**：陈述句语序 + 升调表惊讶反问——"（言下之意）你觉得我不可爱？"；think 后接宾语从句 (that) I'm cute（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1104.84" duration="13.584" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** I, I don't know, um, do you think you're cute? OK, <Note>we're kinda gettin' off the track here</Note>. Um, <Note>I was supposed to come here and tell you my friend thinks you're cute</Note>. So what should I tell her?
> 我，我不知道，嗯，你觉得你可爱吗？好了，我们有点偏离轨道了。我应该来告诉你我朋友觉得你很可爱。那我该怎么告诉她？
<GrammarTip points="短语动词,被动语态">

- **we're kinda gettin' off the track**：**get off the track**"跑题了"；进行式"（咱们）有点聊岔了"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **I was supposed to come here and tell you...**：**be supposed to + 原形**"（本来）应该……"——was supposed to 表"原计划"；tell you 后接宾语从句 (that) my friend thinks...（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1118.424" duration="2.352" /> **VAN DAMME:** <Note>You can tell her I think her friend is cute</Note>.
> 你可以告诉她我觉得她的朋友很可爱。
<GrammarTip points="双宾语,名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">You</span> <span class="v">can tell</span> <span class="o">her</span> <span class="o">(that) I think her friend is cute</span></p>

- **tell sb + that 从句**：双宾语结构；从句内又套 **think (that) her friend is cute** 第二层宾语从句——"她的朋友"指 Rachel，反撩（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[back to Chandler and Susie]
> [回到钱德勒和苏西那里]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1120.776" duration="7.344" /> **CHANDLER BING:** No, Stephen Hurs was <Note>the kid who would eat anything for money</Note>, David Stein was <Note>the guy who had no elbows</Note>.
> 不，斯蒂芬·赫斯是个为了钱什么都吃的孩子，大卫·斯坦是个没有肘部的人。
<GrammarTip points="关系从句,情态动词">

- **the kid who would eat anything for money**：**who 引导定语从句**（台：关系从句）；**would eat** 表过去惯常——"那个为了钱什么都敢吃的孩子"（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **the guy who had no elbows**：who 作主语不可省（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1128.12" duration="3.48" /> **SUSIE:** OK, well then <Note>who was the kid that got caught masturbating</Note>?
> 好吧，那么被抓到手淫的那个孩子是谁？
<GrammarTip points="关系从句,被动语态">

- **the kid that got caught masturbating**：**that 引导定语从句**（台：关系从句）；**get caught + 现在分词**"（做……时）被当场抓住"——get caught 表被动，masturbating 作主语补语（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1131.6" duration="5.928" /> **CHANDLER BING:** OK <Note>that's not what he was doing</Note>. Alright, <Note>he was looking for his bus money</Note>.
> 好吧，他不是这么做的。好吧，他在找他的公车钱。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,动词时态">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">that</span> <span class="v">'s not</span> <span class="c">what he was doing</span></p>

- **what he was doing**：what 引导**表语从句**（台：名词从句）——"他当时在干的不是那档子事"；从句用**过去进行式**（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **he was looking for his bus money**：**过去进行式**还原"案发"真相——"他在找公交卡钱"；look for 短语动词（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[a voice in the background calls for makeup]
> [背景中有声音要求化妆]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1137.528" duration="1.464" /> **SUSIE:** Oh that's me, <Note>I gotta go</Note>.
> 哦，是我，我得走了。
<GrammarTip points="情态动词">

- **I gotta go**：**gotta = have got to** = have to"我得走了"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1138.992" duration="3.048" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Oh uh, o, OK.
> 哦，哦，好的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1142.04" duration="5.232" /> **SUSIE:** Um, so listen, <Note>how many times am I gonna have to touch you on the arm before you ask me on a date</Note>.
> 嗯，听着，在你约我约会之前，我要碰你多少次胳膊。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,副词从句,动词时态">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1142.04" duration="5.232" />

**原句**：How many times am I gonna have to touch you on the arm before you ask me on a date?

**层次划分**：

- 主干：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">am gonna have to touch</span> <span class="o">you</span> <span class="a">on the arm</span></p> **be going to + have to**"得还要……"；how many times 疑问词提前（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- **before you ask me on a date**：**before 引导时间状语从句**（台：副词从句），**现在式 ask 代未来**；ask sb on a date"约某人出去"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **touch you on the arm**：身体部位前用 **the**（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

**翻译**：说吧，在你开口约我之前，还得让我摸你胳膊多少回？

**语法要点**：before 从句现在式代未来（Ch.13）；have to 的将来表达（Ch.3/9）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1147.272" duration="5.976" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Well, uh, let's try one more. . . <Note>there you go</Note>, say Ernie's, 8 o'clock.
> 嗯，让我们再试一次。好了，说厄尼家，8点。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语">

- **there you go**：惯用语"（成了！）就这样"——此处带"得逞"的得意（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1153.248" duration="5.712" /> **SUSIE:** I'll be there, and who knows, <Note>if things go well, maybe this time I'll get to see your underwear</Note>.
> 我会去的，谁知道呢，如果一切顺利，也许这次我可以看看你的内衣。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,不定词">

- **if things go well**：if 条件**状语从句**（台：副词从句），**现在式 go 代未来**（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **I'll get to see your underwear**：**get to + 原形**"有机会/得以……"——"说不定这回我能看到你的内裤"（呼应四年级被掀裙之仇）（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1158.96" duration="2.52" /> **CHANDLER BING:** <Note>No one was around to hear that</Note>?
> 周围没人听到吗？
<GrammarTip points="不定词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">No one</span> <span class="v">was</span> <span class="a">around</span> <span class="a">to hear that</span></p>

- **be around to do**："在场（以至于）听到/看到……"——不定式 to hear 表结果（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **No one 作主语动词用单数** was（[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[back to Rachel and Monica]
> [回到瑞秋和莫妮卡身边]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1161.48" duration="2.112" /> **MONICA GELLER:** So <Note>what'd he say</Note>?
> 那他说什么了？
<GrammarTip points="口语缩略">

- **what'd he say?**：= what did he say?——'d 是 did 的口语缩略（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1163.592" duration="9.528" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Agh, what a jerk. <Note>I kept talking about you and he kept asking me out</Note>. I mean, naturally, you know, I said no.
> 啊，真是个混蛋。我一直在谈论你，他一直在约我出去。我是说，很自然，你知道，我说了不。
<GrammarTip points="动名词,短语动词">

- **I kept talking about you**：**keep + 动名词**"不停地……"（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）
- **he kept asking me out**：keep + V-ing；**ask sb out**"约某人出去"（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **what a jerk**：感叹句 What + (a) + 名词！"真是个混蛋！"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1173.12" duration="2.376" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Well, thanks anyway.
> 不管怎样，谢谢你。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1175.496" duration="6.552" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** He just kept asking, and asking, and asking, and asking, and asking, and asking.
> 他只是不停地问，问，问，问，问，问，问。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1182.048" duration="7.896" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Rachel <Note>if you, if you want to go out with him, you can</Note>. Sound like a big jerk to me but <Note>if that's what you want to do</Note>...
> 瑞秋，如果你，如果你想和他出去，你可以。听起来像个大混蛋，但如果那是你想做的。。。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,名词从句">

- **if you want to go out with him, you can (go out with him)**：if 条件**状语从句**（台：副词从句）**现在式代未来**；主句 can 后省略重复部分（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **(He) sounds like a big jerk to me**：sound like + 名词"听起来像"；口语省略主语（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **if that's what you want to do**：**what you want to do** 是表语从句（台：名词从句）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1189.944" duration="5.04" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Jean-Claude <Note>she said yes</Note>, I'll see you tonight. Thank you.
> 让·克劳德，她答应了，今晚见。非常感谢。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

- **she said (that) yes / (that) she'll see you tonight**：say + 宾语从句；yes 作名词"答应了"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Central Perk. Joey and the girls are sitting at the couches.*
> 🎬 *中央公园。乔伊和女孩们坐在沙发旁。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：where 名词从句 / end up doing / 疑问词 + 不定式 / 完成式

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1194.984" duration="5.64" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** And then Jean-Claude took me to that place Crossroads and <Note>that's where we hung out with Drew Barrymore</Note>.
> 然后让·克劳德带我去了十字路口，那就是我们和德鲁·巴里摩尔在一起的地方。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,短语动词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">that</span> <span class="v">'s</span> <span class="c">where we hung out with Drew Barrymore</span></p>

- **where we hung out with...**：**where 引导表语从句**（台：名词从句）——"那儿就是我们跟德鲁·巴里摩尔混的地方"；从句内陈述语序（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **hang out with sb**：短语动词"与某人厮混"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1200.624" duration="5.04" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Oh man, she's so smokin, <Note>she has got the greatest set of</Note>. . . no guys around, huh.
> 哦，天哪，她真是个烟鬼，她有一套最棒的。周围没有人，嗯。
<GrammarTip points="形容词比较,惯用语">

- **she's got = she has got**：口语"她有"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **the greatest set of...**：**最高级**——话说一半发现没男生在场，自知没趣打住（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1205.664" duration="2.4" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Does anybody need anything?
> 有人需要什么吗？
<GrammarTip points="主谓一致">

- **anybody 作主语，动词用单数** does/need**s**——疑问句中 **主谓一致**（台：主语动词一致性）（[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1208.064" duration="9.192" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Oh, I'll have an espresso. Oh <Note>acutally</Note>, I'll get it. <Note>If I ask you to, you'll probably end up drinking it yourself</Note>.
> 哦，我要一杯浓缩咖啡。哦，我会的。如果我让你喝的话，你可能会自己喝。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,动名词">

- **If I ask you to, you'll probably end up drinking it yourself**：if 条件**状语从句**（台：副词从句）**现在式 ask 代未来**；ask you to (get it) 不定式省略重复；**end up + 动名词**"结果反而……"——"我要是让你去，最后多半是你自己喝掉"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）
- **原文勘误**：`acutally` 应为 **actually**（漏 c）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1217.256" duration="2.232" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** That is so unfair.
> 那太不公平了。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1219.488" duration="4.8" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** I know. Oh, <Note>like you would drink her coffee after what you did to her with Van Damme</Note>.
> 我知道。哦，就像你和范·达姆对她做了什么之后，你会喝她的咖啡一样。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,语气">

- **like you would drink her coffee**：like + 从句"好像你真会喝她的咖啡似的"——**would** 表反讽的假设（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **after what you did to her with Van Damme**：**what 引导名词从句**作介词 after 的宾语——"在你伙同范·达美对她干了那档子事之后"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[Ross enters]
> [罗斯进来]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1224.288" duration="8.448" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Hey Joey <Note>I have to cancel racketball for tonight</Note>, that was Marcel's trainer. <Note>He's gonna let me have him for a couple of hours</Note>.
> 嘿，乔伊，我得取消今晚的球拍，那是马塞尔的教练。他会让我陪他几个小时。
<GrammarTip points="情态动词,使役动词">

- **I have to cancel...**：have to + 原形"不得不取消"；for tonight 介词短语定语（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **He's gonna let me have him for a couple of hours**：**let + 宾语 + 原形 have**（使役动词不加 to）——"他会让我带（马塞尔）几个小时"；**a couple of + 复数名词**"两三个"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1232.736" duration="2.784" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** <Note>You're blowin' me off for a monkey</Note>?
> 你为了一只猴子把我甩了？
<GrammarTip points="短语动词,动词时态">

- **blow sb off**：短语动词"放某人鸽子"；**现在进行式**表"（你这可是正在）放我鸽子"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1235.52" duration="3.288" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Hey, we can <Note>rescedule</Note> for Saturday.
> 嘿，我们可以在星期六休息。
<GrammarTip points="情态动词">

- **can + 原形 reschedule**：can 表提议"我们可以改到周六"；**原文勘误**：`rescedule` 应为 **reschedule**（漏 h）（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1238.808" duration="3.552" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Yeah, <Note>unless you hook up with a bunch of pigeons</Note>.
> 是啊，除非你和一群鸽子混在一起。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,短语动词">

- **unless you hook up with...**：**unless = if...not** 引导条件**状语从句**（台：副词从句）"除非你又勾搭上……"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **hook up with / a bunch of**：hook up with"跟……勾搭上"；a bunch of + 复数"一群"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[Chandler enters]
> [钱德勒进来]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1242.36" duration="3.72" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Hey, <Note>stick a fork in me, I am done</Note>.
> 嘿，给我插把叉子，我完了。
<GrammarTip points="习语,祈使句">

- **Stick a fork in me, I'm done**：惯用语——烤肉时插叉子试生熟，"（往我身上插把叉子吧）我熟透了/我累瘫了"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1246.08" duration="1.536" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Stick a fork what?
> 插叉子什么？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1247.616" duration="2.808" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Like, <Note>when you're cooking a steak</Note>.
> 比如，当你做牛排的时候。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句">

- **when you're cooking a steak**：when 时间**状语从句**（台：副词从句）+ **现在进行式**"煎牛排的时候"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1250.424" duration="1.752" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh, OK, I don't eat meat.
> 哦，好吧，我不吃肉。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1252.176" duration="3.6" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Well then, <Note>how do you know when vegetables are done</Note>?
> 那么，你怎么知道蔬菜什么时候熟？
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">do know</span> <span class="o">when vegetables are done</span></p>

- **when vegetables are done**：**when 引导宾语从句**（台：名词从句）——从句内**陈述语序**（× when are vegetables done）；done"煮熟了"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1255.776" duration="2.976" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Well you know, you <Note>juist</Note>, you eat them and you can tell.
> 嗯，你知道，你是朱伊斯特人，你吃了它们，你就知道了。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语">

- **you can tell**：tell = 分辨、看得出——"一吃就知道"；**原文勘误**：`juist` 应为 **just**（字母颠倒）（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1258.752" duration="3.48" /> **CHANDLER BING:** OK, then, eat me, I'm done.
> 好吧，那么，吃了我，我完了。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1262.232" duration="11.04" /> **CHANDLER BING:** <Note>I've met the perfect woman</Note>. OK, we're sitting on her couch, <Note>we're fooling around</Note>, and then suddenly she turns to me and says, <Note>'Do you ever want to do it in an elevator?'</Note>
> 我遇到了一个完美的女人。好吧，我们坐在她的沙发上，我们在鬼混，然后突然她转向我说，‘你想在电梯里做吗？’
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,动词时态,短语动词">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1262.232" duration="11.04" />

**原句**：I've met the perfect woman. We're sitting on her couch, we're fooling around, and then suddenly she turns to me and says, 'Do you ever want to do it in an elevator?'

**层次划分**：

- **I've met the perfect woman**：**现在完成式**宣布"（最近）我遇到了……"——新鲜出炉的消息（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **we're sitting on her couch, we're fooling around**：叙事切到**现在进行式**——历史现在式让故事如临现场（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **she turns to me and says**：turn to sb"转向某人"；and 并列（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）
- **Do you ever want to do it in an elevator?**：**ever + 现在式**"（平时）有没有想过……"；do it"那事儿"的含蓄说法（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

**翻译**：我遇到了完美女人。那天我们窝在她家沙发上腻歪，她突然转头问我："你想不想在电梯里来一发？"

**语法要点**：完成式报新消息（Ch.3）；历史现在式叙事（Ch.3）；ever + 现在式（Ch.8）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1273.272" duration="1.944" /> **MONICA GELLER:** What did you say?
> 你说什么？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1275.216" duration="9.912" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Ahh, I believe my exact words were, 'Flaign,en - sten'. I mean <Note>I didn't know what to say</Note>, <Note>how do you know if you wanna do it on an elevator</Note>?
> 啊，我想我确切的话是，‘炫耀，听’。我的意思是我不知道该说什么，你怎么知道你想在电梯上做？
<GrammarTip points="不定词,名词从句">

- **I didn't know what to say**：**疑问词 + 不定式**（台：不定词）——"不知道该说什么"；what to say 作 know 的宾语（= what I should say 的简化）（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.18](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter18.md)）
- **how do you know if you wanna do it...?**：know 后接 **if（是否）引导的宾语从句**（台：名词从句），陈述语序（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1285.128" duration="1.344" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh, you just know.
> 哦，你刚刚知道。

---

🎬 *Chandler and Joey's apartment. Chandler and Susie are making out on the couch.*
> 🎬 *钱德勒和乔伊的公寓。钱德勒和苏西在沙发上亲热。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：委婉虚拟 / want sb to do / 所有格 / 完成式 + ever

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1286.472" duration="3.72" /> **SUSIE:** Oh, shoot, <Note>we gotta go, got a reservation in 30 minutes</Note>.
> 噢，天哪，我们得走了，30分钟后预订了房间。
<GrammarTip points="情态动词,省略">

- **we gotta go**：gotta = have got to"得走了"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **(we've) got a reservation in 30 minutes**：口语省略 've；**in + 时段**"……之后"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1290.192" duration="8.568" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Oh no no no no, no no no no no no, you see, <Note>what I had planned shouldn't take more that 2, 3 minutes tops</Note>.
> 哦，不，不，不，不，你看，我的计划不应该超过2，3分钟。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,情态动词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">what I had planned</span> <span class="v">shouldn't take</span> <span class="o">more than 2, 3 minutes</span> <span class="a">tops</span></p>

- **what I had planned**：**what 引导主语从句**（台：名词从句）"我盘算的那档子事"；**过去完成式** had planned"（早就）计划好的"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **shouldn't take more than...**：shouldn't"按理不该"；take 表"花费（时间）"；**tops**"顶多"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **原文勘误**：`more that` 应为 **more than**（比较级用 than）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1298.76" duration="3.072" /> **SUSIE:** Oh, 200 seconds of passion. We gotta go.
> 哦，200秒的激情。我们得走了。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1301.832" duration="4.08" /> **SUSIE:** But um, <Note>here's an idea</Note>, <Note>have you ever worn women's underwear</Note>?
> 但是，嗯，有个主意，你穿过女式内衣吗？
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

- **here's an idea**：惯用语"我有个主意"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **have you ever worn...?**：**现在完成式 + ever**"（至今）有没有穿过……"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1305.912" duration="7.488" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Well, ye, yes, actually, but, uh, <Note>they were my Aunt Edna's</Note>, and <Note>there were three of us in there</Note>.
> 嗯，是的，事实上，但是，呃，他们是我阿姨埃德娜的，我们三个人在里面。
<GrammarTip points="所有格,存在句">

- **they were my Aunt Edna's (underwear)**：所有格 's 后**省略已提到的名词**——"是我埃德娜姨妈的（内裤）"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **there were three of us in there**：There be 存在句复数；**three of us**"我们中的三个人"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1313.4" duration="4.584" /> **SUSIE:** Well <Note>I was thinking it would be um, kind of sexy if you wore mine tonight</Note>, at dinner.
> 我在想，如果你今晚在晚宴上穿我的，那会有点性感。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,虚拟语气,动词时态">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1313.4" duration="4.584" />

**原句**：I was thinking it would be kind of sexy if you wore mine tonight, at dinner.

**层次划分**：

- **I was thinking...**：**过去进行式表委婉**——"我在想啊……"（试探性提议）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- 主干：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">it</span> <span class="v">would be</span> <span class="c">kind of sexy</span></p> **would be** 假设语气"那会很……"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **if you wore mine tonight**：if 条件**状语从句**（台：副词从句）用**过去式 wore**——与现在事实相反的委婉假设；**mine** = my underwear，名词性物主代词（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

**翻译**：我在想啊——今晚吃饭的时候，你要是穿我的（内裤），会不会有点性感？

**语法要点**：was thinking 委婉（Ch.3）；if + 过去式虚拟（Ch.13/9）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1317.984" duration="2.52" /> **CHANDLER BING:** <Note>You want me to wear your panties</Note>?
> 你想让我穿你的内裤？
<GrammarTip points="不定词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">want</span> <span class="o">me</span> <span class="c">to wear your panties</span></p>

- **want + 宾语 + 不定式补语**："你想让我穿你的内裤？"——**不定式**（台：不定词）to wear 作宾语补足语（台：补语）（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1320.504" duration="0.792" /> **SUSIE:** Could ya?
> 可以吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1321.296" duration="6.912" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Well, <Note>if I was wearing your underwear then, uh, what would you be wearing</Note>?. . . You're swell.
> 嗯，如果我穿着你的内衣，那么，呃，你会穿什么。你太棒了。
<GrammarTip points="虚拟语气,动词时态">

- **if I was wearing your underwear, what would you be wearing?**：**if + 过去式（进行式）**表假设；主句 **would be wearing** 过去将来进行式——"那我穿你的，你穿啥？"（口语中 was 代 were）（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Ross's apartment. Ross is setting up for his time with Marcel. Joey is there.*
> 🎬 *罗斯的公寓。罗斯正准备与马塞尔共度时光。乔伊在那里。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：口语省略 / gotta / it's not like 句型

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1328.208" duration="8.208" /> **ROSS GELLER:** OK, <Note>got the music, got the dinner</Note>. Oh and check it out, <Note>I made Marcel's favorite dish, banannacake</Note>.
> 好的，有音乐，有晚餐。哦，看看吧，我做了马塞尔最喜欢的菜，香蕉蛋糕。
<GrammarTip points="省略,最高级">

- **(I've) got the music, (I've) got the dinner**：口语省略 I've——"音乐备好了，晚餐备好了"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **Marcel's favorite dish**：**favorite** 本身即含"最"义，不加 most（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- **原文勘误**：`banannacake` 应为 **banana cake**（banana 只有一个 nn→n）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1336.416" duration="1.368" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Oooh.
> 哦。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1337.784" duration="2.04" /> **ROSS GELLER:** With mealworms.
> 用粉虱。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1339.824" duration="6.648" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Yaaahhh. Ahhh, candles. <Note>What'dya thinks gonna happen here tonight</Note>?
> 是啊。啊，蜡烛。迪娅认为今晚这里会发生什么？
<GrammarTip points="口语缩略,将来时">

- **What'dya (do you) think('s) gonna happen here tonight?**：gonna = going to 表"今晚会发生啥"；**原文勘误**：`thinks` 应为 **think**（'dya = do you，主语是 you）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[phone rings]
> [电话铃响]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1346.472" duration="22.296" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Hello. Oh hi, <Note>are you on your way ove-</Note>. Oh. No, no, I, I understand, I mean <Note>a monkey's gotta work</Note>. No it, <Note>it's no big deal</Note>, <Note>it' not like I uh, had anything special planned</Note>. Yeah OK, OK. OK, OK, bye.
> 你好。哦，嗨，你在去爱的路上吗。哦不，不，我，我明白，我是说猴子要工作。不，没什么大不了的，我没有什么特别的计划。是的，好的，好的。好的，好的，再见。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,惯用语,分词">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1346.472" duration="22.296" />

**原句**：Are you on your way ove-? A monkey's gotta work. It's no big deal, it's not like I had anything special planned.

**层次划分**：

- **are you on your way over (here)?**：**on one's way (over)**"在路上"；**现在进行式表将来**——"你快到了吗？"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **a monkey's gotta work**：**gotta = has got to**"猴子也得开工嘛"——自我安慰（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **it's no big deal**：惯用语"没什么大不了"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **it's not like I had anything special planned**：**It's not like + 从句**"又不是……"；**planned** 是过去分词作 anything special 的后置定语——"（我）又没什么特别的安排"（嘴硬）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)）

**翻译**：喂？哦嗨，你在路上了吗——哦。没事没事，我懂，猴子也得上班嘛。没啥大不了的，反正我也没啥特别的安排。好好，拜拜。

**语法要点**：on one's way（Ch.10）；It's not like + 从句（Ch.12）；分词后置定语（Ch.6）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Monica and Rachel's apartment. Monica and Rachel are upset with each other. Phoebe is mediating.*
> 🎬 *莫妮卡和瑞秋的公寓。莫妮卡和瑞秋彼此不高兴。菲比在调解。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：否定前移 / 强调助动词 did / quit + 动名词 / 虚拟语气

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1368.768" duration="2.736" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** OK, Rachel, <Note>why don't you start talking first</Note>.
> 好吧，瑞秋，你为什么不先开口说话呢。
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,建议">

- **Why don't you + 原形**：建议句型"你何不先说？"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1371.504" duration="13.344" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Alright, <Note>I feel that this is totally unjustified</Note>. [Monica starts making faces behind her back] <Note>She gave me the green light</Note>, I did nothing but-. <Note>Do you think I can't see you in the TV set</Note>?
> 好吧，我觉得这是完全没有道理的。[莫妮卡开始在背后做鬼脸]她给了我绿灯，我什么也没做，只是-。你以为我在电视机里看不到你吗？
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,名词从句,习语">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1371.504" duration="13.344" />

**原句**：I feel that this is totally unjustified. She gave me the green light, I did nothing but-. Do you think I can't see you in the TV set?

**层次划分**：

- **I feel (that) this is totally unjustified**：feel + 宾语从句（台：名词从句）"我觉得这完全没道理"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **She gave me the green light**：习语 **give sb the green light**"给某人开绿灯/放行"；give + 双宾语（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **I did nothing but (go out with him)**：do nothing but + 原形"只不过做了……"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **Do you think (that) I can't see you in the TV set?**：**Do you think + 宾语从句**——从句内 I can't see 陈述语序；in the TV set"在电视屏幕（的反光）里"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）

**翻译**：我觉得这事完全没道理。她给我开了绿灯，我只不过——你以为你在电视机反光里做鬼脸我看不见吗？

**语法要点**：feel/think + 宾语从句（Ch.12）；give sb the green light（Ch.1）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1384.848" duration="3.336" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Alright Monica, <Note>if there is something that you would like to share</Note>...
> 好吧，莫妮卡，如果你有什么想分享的。。。
<GrammarTip points="存在句,关系从句">

- **if there is something (that) you would like to share**：There be 存在句嵌入 if 条件从句；**that 引导定语从句**（台：关系从句）修饰 something，that 作 share 的宾语可省；**would like to + 原形**"想要……"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1388.184" duration="3.336" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Ya know, <Note>you had no right to go out with him</Note>.
> 你知道，你没有权利和他约会。
<GrammarTip points="不定词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">had</span> <span class="o">no right to go out with him</span></p>

- **have no right to do**："没权利做……"——**不定式**（台：不定词）to go out 作 right 的后置定语（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1391.52" duration="2.568" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** <Note>That is the most ridiculous</Note>.
> 那太可笑了。
<GrammarTip points="形容词比较">

- **the most ridiculous (thing I've ever heard)**：最高级后省略名词——"太离谱了"（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1394.088" duration="2.088" /> **MONICA GELLER:** <Note>You sold me out</Note>.
> 你出卖了我。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">You</span> <span class="v">sold</span> <span class="o">me</span> <span class="a">out</span></p>

- **sell sb out**：短语动词"出卖某人"——代词 me 夹中间（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1396.176" duration="2.328" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** <Note>I did not sell you out</Note>.
> 我没有出卖你。
<GrammarTip points="强调">

- **I did not sell you out**：**助动词 did + 原形**——过去式否定靠 did not 构成（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1398.504" duration="3.672" /> **MONICA GELLER:** <Note>Yes you did</Note>. Absolutely.
> 是的。绝对地
<GrammarTip points="强调,省略">

- **Yes you did (sell me out)**：**did 重读表强调**——"你就是出卖了！"；did 替代整个谓语避免重复（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1402.176" duration="4.536" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** <Note>Would you let me talk</Note>. [flicks Monica on the forehead]
> 你能让我谈谈吗。[在莫妮卡的额头上轻弹]
<GrammarTip points="情态动词,使役动词">

- **Would you let me talk?**：would you + 原形"你能不能……"（请求）；**let + 宾语 + 原形 talk**（使役动词不加 to）（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1406.712" duration="2.136" /> **MONICA GELLER:** <Note>Did you just flick me</Note>?
> 你刚才是不是甩了我一下？
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

- **Did you just flick me?**：**just + 过去式**"你刚才是不是弹了我？！"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1408.848" duration="8.52" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** OK, well, <Note>you wouldn't let me finish</Note> and I was jus- [Monica flicks her back] Ow. That hurt [flicks Monica]
> 好吧，你不让我说完，我只是…[莫妮卡弹了弹她的背]哦。痛[弹莫妮卡]
<GrammarTip points="情态动词">

- **you wouldn't let me finish**：**wouldn't** 表"（怎么都）不肯"——过去意愿的否定；let + 宾语 + 原形 finish（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1417.368" duration="2.496" /> **MONICA GELLER:** <Note>Quit flicking</Note> [flicks]
> 别拍电影了
<GrammarTip points="动名词">

- **Quit + 动名词 flicking**："别弹了！"——quit 后接 V-ing（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1419.864" duration="2.52" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Ow, you stop flicking.
> 哦，你别弹了。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1422.384" duration="2.184" /> **MONICA GELLER:** <Note>You flicked me first</Note>.
> 你先甩了我。
<GrammarTip points="副词">

- **first**：副词"先、首先"——"是你先动手的"（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[They keep flicking each other. This turns into slapping each other. This leads to wrestling on the floor. All the while Phoebe is saying "Happy thoughts". Eventually Phoebe gets fed up.]
> [他们不停地互相拍打。这变成了互相扇耳光。这导致了在地板上摔跤。菲比一直在说“快乐的想法”。最终菲比受够了。]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1424.568" duration="2.088" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** OK, now I'm gonna kick some ass.
> 好吧，现在我要踢一些屁股。

[Phoebe grabs each of them by an ear]
> [菲比抓住他们每个人的耳朵]

MONICA AND **RACHEL GREEN: **Ow. Ow. Ow.

> 莫妮卡和**瑞秋·格林：**噢。噢噢

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1426.656" duration="2.664" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Alright, now <Note>I will let go if you both stop</Note>.
> 好吧，现在如果你们都停下来，我就放手了。
<GrammarTip points="副词从句,动词时态">

- **I will let go if you both stop**：主句 **will** 未来式，if 条件**状语从句**（台：副词从句）用**现在式 stop 代未来**（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1429.32" duration="4.968" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Oh, what do you, <Note>you want me to stop seeing him</Note>, <Note>is that what you want</Note>?
> 哦，你想让我不要再见到他，这就是你想要的吗？
<GrammarTip points="不定词,动名词,名词从句">

- **you want me to stop seeing him**：**want + 宾语 + 不定式补语**（台：不定词/补语）；**stop + 动名词** seeing"别再跟他来往"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）
- **is that what you want?**：**what 引导表语从句**（台：名词从句）"这就是你要的吗？"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1434.288" duration="1.488" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Uh-huh.
> 嗯。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1435.776" duration="6.024" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** <Note>You want me to just call him up and tell him that you're seeing him instead</Note>? That's what you want?
> 你想让我给他打个电话，告诉他你要去见他？这就是你想要的？
<GrammarTip points="短语动词,名词从句,动词时态">

- **call him up**：短语动词"给他打电话"，代词夹中间（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **tell him (that) you're seeing him instead**：tell + 双宾 + 宾语从句；从句用**现在进行式** are seeing——"你（现在正）跟他好上了"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1441.8" duration="1.608" /> **MONICA GELLER:** OK.
> 好的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1443.408" duration="2.208" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Oh that's what you want.
> 哦，那就是你想要的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1445.616" duration="1.608" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Yes.
> 是的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1447.224" duration="1.512" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Fine.
> 好的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1448.736" duration="1.56" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Fine,
> 好的，

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1450.296" duration="4.992" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** <Note>There we go</Note>.You know what, <Note>if we were in prison, you guys would be, like, my bitches</Note>.
> 好了。你知道吗，如果我们在监狱里，你们会像我的婊子一样。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,虚拟语气">

- **There we go**：惯用语"（好了）搞定了"（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- **if we were in prison, you guys would be my bitches**：**与现在事实相反的虚拟**——if + 过去式 were，主句 would be——"咱俩要是在坐牢，你们俩就是我的人了"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *A fancy restraunt (Marcel's). Joey, Ross, Chandler, Susie, and the Director's Assistant are there.*
> 🎬 *一个漂亮的姑妈（马塞尔的）。乔伊、罗斯、钱德勒、苏西和导演助理都在那里。*

<GrammarTip points="原文勘误">

- ⚠️ 场景描述原文 `restraunt` 应为 **restaurant**；中译"姑妈"为机翻错误

</GrammarTip>

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：动名词 / have + 宾语 + 分词 / 虚拟假设 / 完成进行式

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1455.288" duration="3.504" /> **ROSS GELLER:** <Note>Thanks for letting me tag along</Note> tonight you guys.
> 谢谢你们今晚让我陪你们。
<GrammarTip points="动名词,短语动词">

- **Thanks for + 动名词**："谢谢你们让我蹭饭"——for 是介词（台：介系词），后接 V-ing；**let + 宾语 + 原形 tag**（使役）（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **tag along**：短语动词"跟着去、蹭着同行"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1458.792" duration="2.016" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Forget about it.
> 算了吧。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1460.808" duration="1.512" /> **SUSIE:** How you doin there squirmy?
> 你在那里过得怎么样？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1462.32" duration="2.88" /> **CHANDLER BING:** <Note>I'm hangin in</Note>. . . and a little out.
> 我在。还有一点点。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词">

- **hang in (there)**：短语动词"撑着、顶住"——Chandler 穿着女式内裤赴宴，自嘲 hangin' in...and a little out（挂里头……还有点露外头）（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1465.2" duration="8.424" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** So, assistant to the director. That's a really exciting job, I mean, <Note>you must have a ton of cool responsibilities</Note>.
> 那么，导演助理。那真是一份令人兴奋的工作，我的意思是，你一定有很多很酷的责任。
<GrammarTip points="情态动词,名词词组">

- **must have**：**must 表推测**"（你）肯定有……"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **a ton of + 复数名词**：口语"一大堆"（= a lot of）（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

DIRECTOR'S ASSISTANT: <Note>I have nothing to do with casting</Note>.
> 导演助理：我和演员无关。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语">

- **have nothing to do with**："与……无关"——一句话堵死 Joey 的套磁（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1473.624" duration="2.616" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** So what're you guys gonna eat?
> 你们要吃什么？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1476.24" duration="4.512" /> **SUSIE:** <Note>How come all I can think about is putting that ice in my mouth and licking you all over</Note>?
> 为什么我能想到的就是把冰放进我嘴里，然后舔你全身？
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,关系从句,动名词">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1476.24" duration="4.512" />

**原句**：How come all I can think about is putting that ice in my mouth and licking you all over?

**层次划分**：

- **How come + 陈述语序**："怎么搞的……"——不倒装（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- 主干：<p class="svoc"><span class="s">all (that) I can think about</span> <span class="v">is</span> <span class="c">putting...and licking...</span></p> **all (that) I can think about**：that 引导定语从句（台：关系从句）修饰 all，that 作 about 的宾语故省略（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）
- **putting that ice in my mouth and licking you all over**：两个**动名词**短语并列作补语——"满脑子就是把冰块含进嘴里、把你从头到脚舔一遍"（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)/[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

**翻译**：怎么搞的，我满脑子想的都是把那块冰含进嘴里，然后把你全身舔个遍？

**语法要点**：How come 不倒装（Ch.22）；all (that)...（Ch.14）；动名词并列（Ch.5）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1480.752" duration="4.536" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Because I went to an all boys high school and <Note>God is making up for it</Note>.
> 因为我上的是一所全男生高中，上帝正在弥补这一点。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词,动词时态">

- **an all boys high school**：all + 复数名词作定语"全男校"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **God is making up for it**：**现在进行式**"（上帝这会儿）正在补偿我"；**make up for**"弥补"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1485.288" duration="2.04" /> **SUSIE:** I want you right here, right now.
> 我要你现在就在这里。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1487.328" duration="8.784" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Right now, right here. <Note>Don't ya think we're in kind of a public plaaaa</Note> [Susie grabs him under the table] <Note>They do have the shrimp</Note>.
> 现在，就在这里。难道你不认为我们是在一个公共场所吗？他们有虾。
<GrammarTip points="否定疑问句,强调">
