- **Don't ya think (that) we're in kind of a public place?**：**否定疑问句**表确认——"你不觉得咱们这是在公共场合吗？"（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **They do have the shrimp**：**do + 原形表强调**——"（这家）还真有虾"（被摸得语无伦次后的强行转移话题）（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1496.112" duration="2.928" /> **SUSIE:** Meet me in the bathroom. [she leaves for the bathroom]
> 在浴室等我。[她去洗手间]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1499.04" duration="4.68" /> **CHANDLER BING:** I'm going to the bathroom now. [leaves for the bathroom]
> 我现在要去洗手间。[去洗手间]

[In the bathroom Susie and Chandler are kissing. She backs into a stall.]
> [在浴室，苏西和钱德勒在接吻。她回到了一个隔间。]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1503.72" duration="0.84" /> **SUSIE:** C'mon.
> 来吧。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1504.56" duration="2.712" /> **CHANDLER BING:** I can't believe we're doing this.
> 我真不敢相信我们会这么做。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1507.272" duration="2.856" /> **SUSIE:** Alright mister, let's see those panties.
> 好的，先生，让我们看看那些内裤。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1510.128" duration="5.472" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Alrighty. [we see Chandler's pants drop from under the stall door]
> 好的。[我们看到钱德勒的裤子从货摊门下掉了下来]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1515.6" duration="3.264" /> **SUSIE:** Ooh. Ooh. But ya know <Note>what would be even sexier</Note>?
> 哦。哦。但你知道什么会更性感吗？
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,形容词比较">

- **what would be even sexier**：**what 引导主语从句**（台：名词从句）"（你猜）什么会更性感？"；**would be + 比较级** sexier 表假设（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1518.864" duration="1.464" /> **CHANDLER BING:** What?
> 什么？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1520.328" duration="2.016" /> **SUSIE:** <Note>If you didn't have your shirt tucked into them</Note>.
> 如果你没有把衬衫塞进里面。
<GrammarTip points="虚拟语气,分词">

- **If you didn't have your shirt tucked into them**：**if + 过去式**表假设；**have + 宾语 + 过去分词 tucked**——"（要是）你的衬衫没塞在内裤里（就好了）"；tuck into"塞进"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1522.344" duration="1.368" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Oh.
> 哦。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1523.712" duration="4.632" /> **SUSIE:** Alright. Now <Note>I would like to see you wearing nothing but them</Note>. <Note>Take your clothes off</Note>.
> 好的。现在我只想看到你穿着它们。脱掉你的衣服。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,感官动词">

- **I would like to see you wearing nothing but them**：**would like to + 原形**"我想……"；**see + 宾语 + 现在分词 wearing**（感官动词句型）；**nothing but + 名词**"除了……什么都不"——"我要看你只穿内裤"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- **Take your clothes off**：短语动词 take off"脱掉"——宾语是名词可前可后（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1528.344" duration="5.856" /> **CHANDLER BING:** OK, but uh, I hope you realize <Note>this means we're gonna miss hearing about the specials</Note>.
> 好吧，但是，我希望你意识到这意味着我们会错过关于特价菜的消息。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,动名词">

- **I hope (that) you realize (that)...**：hope + 宾语从句，从句内又套 realize + 宾语从句（台：名词从句）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **we're gonna miss hearing about the specials**：**miss + 动名词**"错过……"；hear about"听说"——"咱们要错过今日特餐介绍喽"（[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1534.2" duration="1.656" /> **SUSIE:** C'mon hurry, hurry.
> 快点，快点。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1535.856" duration="4.416" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Hey, <Note>do you want this done quick, or do you want this done right</Note>?
> 嘿，你是想快点做完，还是想把它做好？
<GrammarTip points="分词,选择疑问句">

- **want + 宾语 + 过去分词 done**："你要这事儿办得快，还是办得好？"——done 作**宾语补足语**（台：补语）表被动/完成；quick/right 此处作副词（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- or 连接的选择疑问句（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1540.272" duration="2.952" /> **SUSIE:** Alright, turn around. <Note>Time to see you from behind</Note>.
> 好的，转过身来。是时候从后面看你了。
<GrammarTip points="不定词">

- **(It's) time to + 原形**："是时候……了"——不定式 to see 作 time 的后置定语；from behind"从后面"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1543.224" duration="1.56" /> **CHANDLER BING:** OK.
> 好的。

[She turns him facing the toilet and sneaks out of the stall and gathers up his clothes.]
> [她把他转向厕所，偷偷溜出摊位，收拾他的衣服。]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1544.784" duration="2.808" /> **SUSIE:** Oh, <Note>somebody's been doing his buns of steel video</Note>.
> 哦，有人在拍他的《钢铁面包》录像。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">somebody</span> <span class="v">'s been doing</span> <span class="o">his buns of steel video</span></p>

- **'s been doing = has been doing**：**现在完成进行式**——"（看来某人）一直在练《钢铁翘臀》操啊"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1547.592" duration="7.728" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Well, <Note>you want me to uh, clench anything</Note>, or-... Susie? Susie.
> 嗯，你想让我，嗯，握紧任何东西，或者-。。。苏西？苏西。
<GrammarTip points="不定词">

- **you want me to clench anything?**：**want + 宾语 + 不定式补语**（台：不定词/补语）——"要我把什么夹紧吗？"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1555.32" duration="1.656" /> **SUSIE:** <Note>This is for the fourth grade</Note>.
> 这是四年级的。
<GrammarTip points="介词">

- **This is for the fourth grade**：**for 表"为了（报）……（之仇）"**——"这一下是替四年级讨的"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1556.976" duration="3.6" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Huh? Where, whaddya mean?
> 嗯？在哪里，你什么意思？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1560.576" duration="5.808" /> **SUSIE:** Whaddo I mean. Whaddya mean, whaddo I mean? I mean underpants, mister, <Note>that's what I mean</Note>.
> 我是什么意思。你是什么意思，我是什么意思？我是说内裤，先生，这就是我的意思。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">that</span> <span class="v">'s</span> <span class="c">what I mean</span></p>

- **what I mean**：what 引导**表语从句**（台：名词从句）——"我就是这个意思"；what = the thing that（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1566.384" duration="2.352" /> **CHANDLER BING:** What, what's what you mean?
> 什么，你什么意思？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1568.736" duration="5.16" /> **SUSIE:** My skirt, you lifted, <Note>kids laughing</Note>. <Note>I was Susie Underpants 'till I was 18</Note>.
> 我的裙子，你撩起，孩子们在笑。我18岁以前一直是苏茜的内裤。
<GrammarTip points="独立结构,副词从句">

- **kids laughing**：**名词 + 现在分词的独立结构**——画面速写"孩子们哄堂大笑"（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md)）
- **I was Susie Underpants 'till I was 18**：**'till = until** 引导时间**状语从句**（台：副词从句）——"我到 18 岁前一直被人叫'内裤苏西'"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1573.896" duration="5.184" /> **CHANDLER BING:** That was in the fourth grade. <Note>How could you still be upset about that</Note>?
> 那是在四年级。你怎么还为此感到不安？
<GrammarTip points="情态动词,介词">

- **How could you still be upset about that?**：**could** 表"（怎么）可能"；**be upset about**"为……耿耿于怀"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1579.08" duration="6.744" /> **SUSIE:** Well um, <Note>why don't you call me in 20 years and tell me if you're still upset about this</Note>. [she leaves with his clothes]
> 嗯，20年后你为什么不给我打个电话，告诉我你是否还在为此烦恼。[她带着他的衣服离开]
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,名词从句">

- **Why don't you call me in 20 years and (why don't you) tell me...?**：建议句型的反讽用法；**in 20 years**"二十年后"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **tell me if you're still upset about this**：**if（是否）引导宾语从句**（台：名词从句）——"看看你这口气能不能咽二十年"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1585.824" duration="4.488" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Alright, I hope you realize <Note>you're not getting these underpants back</Note>.
> 好吧，我希望你意识到你没有把这些内裤拿回来。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词,动词时态">

- **you're not getting these underpants back**：**get sth back**"拿回某物"；**现在进行式表（未来的）必然**——"这内裤你是要不回去喽"（嘴硬挽尊）（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Monica and Van Damme are walking down the street.*
> 🎬 *莫妮卡和范达姆在街上散步。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：时态对比 / agree to do / 使役动词 / be dying to do

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1590.312" duration="12.504" /> **MONICA GELLER:** I can't believe this, just like 2 weeks ago <Note>I was watching Sudden Death</Note>, now <Note>I'm on a date with Jean-Claude Van Damme</Note>. <Note>Can you beat up that guy</Note>? [he nods] Can you beat up that guy?
> 我真不敢相信，就像两周前我在看《猝死》一样，现在我在和让·克劳德·范达姆约会。你能打败那个家伙吗？你能打败那个家伙吗？
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,动词时态,短语动词">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1590.312" duration="12.504" />

**原句**：Just like 2 weeks ago I was watching Sudden Death, now I'm on a date with Jean-Claude Van Damme. Can you beat up that guy?

**层次划分**：

- **2 weeks ago I was watching Sudden Death**：**ago + 过去进行式**——"两周前我还在看《突然死亡》"；ago 是过去式标志（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）
- **now I'm on a date with Jean-Claude Van Damme**：**be on a date with sb**"在跟某人约会"——过去看碟、现在约会本人，时态对比制造梦幻感（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **Can you beat up that guy?**：**can 表能力**；**beat up** 短语动词"痛扁"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

**翻译**：真不敢相信——两周前我还在看《突然死亡》的碟，现在居然在跟让-克劳德·范·达美本人约会。你能放倒那家伙吗？那个呢？

**语法要点**：ago + 过去式（Ch.3）；be on a date with（Ch.10）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1602.816" duration="0.864" /> **VAN DAMME:** Sure.
> 当然。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1603.68" duration="7.848" /> **MONICA GELLER:** This is so wild. Ya know, I gotta admit, <Note>I was kinda surprised that you agreed to go on a blind date</Note>.
> 这太疯狂了。你知道，我得承认，我有点惊讶你同意去相亲。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,不定词">

- **I gotta admit (that)...**：gotta = have got to"我得承认"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **I was kinda surprised (that) you agreed to go on a blind date**：**surprised + that 从句**（台：名词从句）——"有点惊讶你居然肯来相亲"；**agree + 不定式** to go"同意做……"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1611.528" duration="1.968" /> **VAN DAMME:** Normally, <Note>I would not do it</Note>.
> 通常，我不会这么做。
<GrammarTip points="情态动词">

- **Normally, I would not do it**：**would** 表"（一般情况下）是不会的"——惯常/假设意味（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1613.496" duration="3.336" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Well, <Note>what made you make the exception for me</Note>?
> 是什么让你为我破例？
<GrammarTip points="使役动词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">what</span> <span class="v">made</span> <span class="o">you</span> <span class="c">make the exception for me</span></p>

- **make + 宾语 + 原形补语**：外层 **made you make...**"是什么让你（愿意）破例的？"——疑问词 what 作主语（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)）
- **make the exception for sb**："为某人破例"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1616.832" duration="8.544" /> **VAN DAMME:** 'Cause Rachel told me uh, <Note>you were dying to have a threesome with me</Note> and uh, Drew Barrymore. By the way, Drew has some groundrules and...
> 因为瑞秋告诉我，你很想和我和德鲁·巴里摩尔一起玩三人行。顺便说一下，Drew有一些基本规则和。。。
<GrammarTip points="双宾语,不定词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">Rachel</span> <span class="v">told</span> <span class="o">me</span> <span class="o">(that) you were dying to have a threesome...</span></p>

- **tell sb + that 从句**：双宾语结构（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **be dying to do**："渴望死了要做……"——**不定式**（台：不定词）to have；从句用过去进行式 were dying 与 told 时态呼应（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

---

🎬 *Back at Monica and Rachel's apartment. They are now fighting about what Rachel told Van Damme.*
> 🎬 *回到莫妮卡和瑞秋的公寓。他们正在为瑞秋告诉范·达姆的事争吵。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：祈使句 / or 表"否则" / too...to / 名词从句

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1625.376" duration="1.968" /> **MONICA GELLER:** <Note>Say you're sorry</Note>.
> 说声对不起。
<GrammarTip points="祈使句,名词从句">

- **Say (that) you're sorry**：祈使句 + 省略 that 的宾语从句——"说'对不起'"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1627.344" duration="2.448" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** No. [hitting each other]
> 没有[打对方]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1629.792" duration="1.68" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Say it.
> 说吧。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1631.472" duration="2.4" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** No. [hitting again]
> 没有[又打了]

[Monica grabs Rachel by the sweater. Rachel squirms out of it]
> [莫妮卡抓住瑞秋的毛衣，瑞秋扭动着从毛衣里出来]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1633.872" duration="3.888" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Rachel, you say you're sorry <Note>or your sweater gets it</Note>.
> 瑞秋，你说对不起，或者你的毛衣收到了。
<GrammarTip points="对等连接词,惯用语">

- **祈使句 + or + 陈述句**："道歉，**否则**……"——or 表"否则"（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）
- **your sweater gets it**：**get it** 俚语"遭殃"——"你的毛衣就要倒霉了"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1637.76" duration="5.232" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** OK, OK, that is my favorite sweater, that is my third date sweater.
> 好的，好的，那是我最喜欢的毛衣，那是我第三次约会穿的毛衣。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1642.992" duration="1.968" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Say you're sorry.
> 说声对不起。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1644.96" duration="9.216" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** OK, you wanna play? OK, let's play, let's play. [She grabs a jar of tomato sauce and Monica's purse]
> 好的，你想玩吗？好的，我们玩吧，我们玩吧。[她抓起一罐番茄酱和莫妮卡的钱包]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1654.176" duration="2.04" /> **MONICA GELLER:** What're you gonna do?
> 你打算怎么办？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1656.216" duration="3.792" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** <Note>You give me back my sweater or it's handbag marinara</Note>.
> 你把我的毛衣还给我，还是把手提包还给玛丽娜。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词,对等连接词">

- **give me back my sweater / give my sweater back (to me)**：短语动词 **give back**"归还"；give + 双宾语（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **or it's handbag marinara**：**or 表"否则"**——"不然你的包就变 marinara（番茄酱汁）手袋了"（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1660.008" duration="2.208" /> **MONICA GELLER:** <Note>You don't have the guts</Note>.
> 你没有勇气。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语">

- **have the guts (to do)**："有种（做）……"——gut 常用复数 guts 表"胆量"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1662.216" duration="6.312" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Oh yeah. Well, <Note>at least I wasn't too chicken to tell some guy I thought he was cute</Note>.
> 哦，是的。好吧，至少我不是胆小鬼，不会告诉别人我觉得他很可爱。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,名词从句">

- **too chicken to tell some guy...**：**too + 形容词 + 不定式**"太……以至于不敢……"；chicken 俚语"胆小鬼"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)）
- **tell some guy (that) I thought he was cute**：tell + 双宾 + 宾语从句；从句内 **I thought (that) he was cute** 又套一层（台：名词从句）（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[Monica pulls a thread on Rachel's sweater and Rachel dumps the tomato sauce in Monica's purse]
> [莫妮卡在瑞秋的毛衣上拉了一根线，瑞秋把番茄酱倒在莫妮卡的钱包里]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1668.528" duration="7.08" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Oh alright, stop, STOP THE MADNESS. This is crazy. <Note>Who can even remember why this even started in the first place</Note>?
> 哦，好吧，停止，停止疯狂。这太疯狂了。谁还记得当初为什么会这样？
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,惯用语">

- **who can remember why this started...?**：**why 引导宾语从句**（台：名词从句），从句内**陈述语序**——"谁还记得这事儿到底怎么闹起来的？"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **in the first place**：惯用语"一开始、当初"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[Monica and Rachel start yelling at the same time]
> [莫妮卡和瑞秋同时大喊大叫]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1675.608" duration="5.448" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Yes that's right. But still, I-, look at your purse, look at your sweater, <Note>look at yourselves</Note>.
> 是的，没错。但是，我，看看你的钱包，看看你的毛衣，看看你自己。
<GrammarTip points="祈使句,反身代词">

- **look at your purse, look at your sweater, look at yourselves**：三个祈使句排比（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）
- **yourselves**：**反身代词**——"你们俩自己（这副样子）"（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1681.056" duration="2.472" /> **MONICA GELLER:** I'll help you fix your sweater.
> 我会帮你修毛衣。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1683.528" duration="2.544" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** <Note>I'll help you throw out your purse</Note>.
> 我会帮你扔掉钱包。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,短语动词">

- **help + 宾语 + (to) 原形**："帮你……"——help you (to) throw out；**throw out**"扔掉"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1686.072" duration="3.144" /> **MONICA GELLER:** <Note>I'm sorry that I made you stop seeing him</Note>.
> 我很抱歉让你不再见到他了。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,使役动词,动名词">

- **I'm sorry (that) I made you stop seeing him**：**sorry + that 从句**（台：名词从句）表道歉的原因；**make + 宾语 + 原形 stop**（使役不加 to）；**stop + 动名词** seeing"停止见……"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1689.216" duration="3.864" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Well, <Note>I'm sorry I went out with him when I knew you liked him</Note>.
> 很抱歉，我知道你喜欢他，就跟他出去了。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,副词从句">

- **I'm sorry (that) I went out with him**：sorry + 省略 that 的宾语从句（台：名词从句）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **when I knew you liked him**：**when 引导时间状语从句**（台：副词从句）；从句内 knew (that) you liked him 又一层宾语从句——"明知你喜欢他还……"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1693.08" duration="5.256" /> **MONICA GELLER:** I'm sorry that I borrowed your gloves [pulls Rachel's gloves out of her purse]
> 很抱歉我借了你的手套[把瑞秋的手套从钱包里拿出来]

---

🎬 *Back at the restroom at Marcel's. Chandler is still in the stall, Joey comes in to go to the bathroom.*
> 🎬 *回到Marcel's的洗手间。Chandler还在隔间里，Joey进来上厕所。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：短语动词 take off / the first time / 进行式表决心 / one of 结构

[Joey starts whistling, Chandler finishes the tune. Joey whistles again.]
> [乔伊吹口哨，钱德勒吹完曲子，乔伊又吹口哨。]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1698.336" duration="1.464" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Joey?
> 乔伊？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1699.8" duration="1.512" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Ma?
> 妈妈？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1701.312" duration="1.536" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Joey!
> 乔伊！

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1702.848" duration="5.208" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Chandler? <Note>What're you still doin' here</Note>, <Note>I though you guys took off</Note>.
> 钱德勒？你们还在这里干什么，我还以为你们走了呢。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,短语动词">

- **What're you still doin' here?**：**现在进行式**——"你怎么还赖在这儿？"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **I thought (that) you guys took off**：think 后省略 that 的宾语从句；**take off**"离开、闪人"；**原文勘误**：`though` 应为 **thought**（漏 t）（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1708.056" duration="4.224" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Oh, no no no, <Note>she took off with my clothes</Note>.
> 哦，不，不，她拿着我的衣服走了。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词">

- **take off with + 名词**："带着……跑了"——跟 take off"离开"相比多了"顺手带走"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1712.28" duration="2.16" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Are you naked in there?
> 你在里面裸体吗？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1714.44" duration="4.176" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Not exactly. . . I'm wearin panties.
> 不完全是。我穿着内裤。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1718.616" duration="3.216" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Huh, you uh, <Note>you always wear panties</Note>?
> 嗯，你总是穿内裤？
<GrammarTip points="副词,动词时态">

- **you always wear panties?**：**always + 现在式**表习惯——"你平时都穿女式内裤？"；陈述语序 + 升调表惊讶（[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1721.832" duration="3.312" /> **CHANDLER BING:** No, no, <Note>this is the first time</Note>.
> 不，不，这是第一次。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语">

- **This is the first time**：惯用语——后接从句时常用完成式（This is the first time (that) I've worn them）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1725.144" duration="6.384" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Wow, <Note>talk about your bad luck</Note>, I mean, <Note>the first time you try panties and someone walks off with your clothes</Note>.
> 哇，说说你的坏运气吧，我是说，当你第一次尝试内裤时，有人拿着你的衣服走开了。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,副词从句,短语动词">

- **Talk about + 名词**：惯用语"说到……（可真够……的）"——"要说倒霉啊"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- **the first time you try panties**：**the first time + 从句** = 时间**状语从句**（台：副词从句）"第一次穿的时候"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）
- **someone walks off with your clothes**：**walk off with**"顺手拿走"；and 连接两个现在式分句（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1731.528" duration="4.008" /> **CHANDLER BING:** <Note>I was not trying them out</Note>, <Note>Susie asked me to wear them</Note>.
> 我不是在试穿，苏茜让我穿。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词,不定词">

- **try them out**：短语动词 **try out**"试穿/试用"，代词 them 夹中间（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **Susie asked me to wear them**：**ask + 宾语 + 不定式**（台：不定词）——"是苏西叫我穿的"（[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1735.536" duration="2.28" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Well, let me see.
> 好吧，让我想想。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1737.816" duration="5.088" /> **CHANDLER BING:** No. <Note>I'm not letting you or anybody else see, ever</Note>.
> 不，我不会让你或任何人看到的，永远不会。
<GrammarTip points="使役动词,动词时态">

- **I'm not letting you or anybody else see (them)**：**let + 宾语 + 原形 see**（使役不加 to）；**现在进行式表坚决的态度**——"我永远不给任何人看"；ever 句尾加强（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1742.904" duration="7.056" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Alright, alright. [climbs up in the next stall and looks over at Chandler] Woah, <Note>someone's flossing</Note>.
> 好的，好的。[爬到下一个摊位，看着钱德勒]哇，有人在用牙线。
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,双关">

- **someone's flossing = someone is flossing**：**现在进行式**；floss"用牙线洁牙"——Joey 毒舌：那丁字裤细得像牙线（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[Ross enters, sees Joey in the mirror]
> [罗斯进来，在镜子里看到乔伊]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1749.96" duration="5.568" /> **ROSS GELLER:** [to Joey who's looking over a toilet stall] Joey, some people don't like that.
> （对正在看厕所的乔伊）乔伊，有些人不喜欢这样。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1755.528" duration="2.544" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Chandler's wearing panties.
> 钱德勒穿着内裤。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1758.072" duration="6.024" /> **ROSS GELLER:** What? Let me see. [climbs up in the other adjoining stall]
> 什么？我想一下。[爬到相邻的另一个摊位上]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1764.096" duration="3.168" /> **CHANDLER BING:** No, no, <Note>you don't have to see</Note>.
> 不，不，你不必看。
<GrammarTip points="情态动词">

- **you don't have to see**：**don't have to = 不必**（≠ must not"不准"）（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1767.264" duration="1.848" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Hi Tushie.
> 嗨，塔西。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1769.112" duration="3.408" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Alright, <Note>one of you give me your underpants</Note>.
> 好吧，你们中的一个把内裤给我。
<GrammarTip points="名词词组">

- **one of + 复数代词/名词**："你们中的一个"——one of you（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）
- 祈使句省略主语 (Will) one of you give me...（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1772.52" duration="2.904" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Can't help you, <Note>I'm not wearing any</Note>.
> 我帮不了你，我没穿。
<GrammarTip points="代词">

- **I'm not wearing any (underwear)**：**any 作代词**"（一点都）没穿"——否定句中用 any（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1775.424" duration="2.952" /> **CHANDLER BING:** <Note>How can you not be wearing any underwear</Note>?
> 你怎么能不穿内衣？
<GrammarTip points="疑问句,动词时态">

- **How can you not be wearing any underwear?**：**can + not + 进行式**——"你怎么能（这会儿）真空上阵？"；疑问词 how 提前倒装（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1778.376" duration="3.816" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Oh, <Note>I'm gettin' heat from the guy in the hot pink thong</Note>.
> 噢，我从那个穿粉红色丁字裤的家伙那里得到了热量。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词,介词">

- **get heat from sb**："被某人怼/挨批"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **the guy in the hot pink thong**：**in + 衣物**"穿着……的"作后置定语——"穿艳粉丁字裤这位"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1782.192" duration="4.272" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Alright look Ross <Note>I'll give you 50 dollars for your underpants</Note>.
> 好吧，罗斯，我给你50美元买你的内裤。
<GrammarTip points="双宾语,介词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">'ll give</span> <span class="o">you</span> <span class="o">50 dollars</span> <span class="a">for your underpants</span></p>

- **give sb sth for sth**：双宾语 + for 表交换——"我出 50 刀买你的内裤"（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[Some guy has entered.]
> [有人进来了。]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1786.464" duration="0.768" /> **ALL:** Hi.
> 大家好。

[Back at the table. Joey and Ross return. Shortly, Chandler comes out, holding the stall door in front of him, and leaves.]
> [回到桌子旁。乔伊和罗斯回来了。不久，钱德勒拿着他面前的摊位门出来，离开了。]

---

🎬 *Central Perk. Ross, Phoebe, and Chandler are sitting.*
> 🎬 *中央公园。罗斯、菲比和钱德勒坐着。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：惯用语双关 / call in sick / 完成式 + just / 介词

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1787.232" duration="3.432" /> **CHANDLER BING:** Hey Phoebs, <Note>can I have the milk after you</Note>?
> 嘿，菲比，我能在你之后喝牛奶吗？
<GrammarTip points="介词,惯用语">

- **after you**："等你用完之后"——**after + 人**表顺序"跟在……后面"；after you 单独用也是礼貌用语"您先请"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1790.664" duration="2.736" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** <Note>I'm almost done with it</Note>, <Note>keep your panties on</Note>.
> 我快做完了，穿上你的内裤。
<GrammarTip points="惯用语,双关">

- **be done with + 名词**："用完/喝完……"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **keep your panties on**：习语 **keep your pants/panties on**"别急嘛、沉住气"——此处顺便调侃 Chandler 的内裤梗，一语双关（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[Joey enters]
> [乔伊进来]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1793.4" duration="3.024" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Hey, hey, and <Note>I'm in the movie</Note>.
> 嘿，嘿，我也在电影里。
<GrammarTip points="介词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">'m</span> <span class="c">in the movie</span></p>

- **be in the movie**：in 表"参演（电影）"——"我也上镜啦"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1796.424" duration="1.704" /> **ROSS GELLER:** What happened?
> 发生什么事了？

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1798.128" duration="11.568" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** <Note>One of the virus victims called in sick</Note>, so Cathy recommended me and boom, <Note>I'm dying on the gurney</Note>. Oh Ross, <Note>Marcel just finished his last scene if you want to go down there and say goodbye</Note>.
> 一个病毒感染者打电话请病假，所以凯茜推荐了我，砰的一声，我要死在轮床上了。哦，罗斯，如果你想下去和他道别的话，马赛尔刚刚结束了他的最后一场戏。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,短语动词,动词时态">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1798.128" duration="11.568" />

**原句**：One of the virus victims called in sick, so Cathy recommended me and boom, I'm dying on the gurney. Marcel just finished his last scene if you want to go down there and say goodbye.

**层次划分**：

- **One of the virus victims called in sick**：**one of + 复数名词**作主语、动词用单数；**call in sick**"打电话请病假"（[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **so Cathy recommended me and boom, I'm dying on the gurney**：so 连接结果分句；**boom** 拟声词"说时迟那时快"；**现在进行式 I'm dying**——"（接下来）我要死在担架床上喽"（进行式表将来安排）（[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **Marcel just finished his last scene**：**just + 过去式/完成式**"刚拍完"（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **if you want to go down there and say goodbye**：if 条件**状语从句**（台：副词从句）**现在式代未来**；go down there"去那边"（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

**翻译**：有个演病毒受害者的打电话请了病假，凯茜就推荐了我——嘭！我要死在担架床上了。对了罗斯，马塞尔刚拍完最后一场戏，你要想去道别就下去吧。

**语法要点**：call in sick（Ch.10）；进行式表安排（Ch.3）；one of + 单数动词（Ch.11）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1809.696" duration="13.056" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Ahh, oh that's OK, I mean, <Note>he's probably got, you know, parties to go to and stuff</Note>. But, ya know, <Note>he's moved on</Note>. Hey, that, <Note>that's the way it goes</Note> right.
> 啊，没关系，我是说，他可能有派对等。但是，你知道，他已经离开了。嘿，那，那是正确的方式。
<GrammarTip points="不定词,短语动词,惯用语">

- **he's probably got parties to go to and stuff**：**has got + 名词 + 不定式定语**——"他有派对要赶什么的"；**to go to** 作 parties 的后置定语（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）
- **he's moved on**：**move on**"（人生）翻篇了"；完成式表现状（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）
- **that's the way it goes**：惯用语"事情就是这样喽"——**the way (that) it goes** 定语从句（台：关系从句）省略关系词（[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1822.752" duration="3.408" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** [sees Marcel at the window] Oh my God.
> （看见马赛尔在窗口）哦，我的天啊。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1826.16" duration="1.44" /> **ROSS GELLER:** What?
> 什么？

["Looks Like We Made It" starts playing and we enter a whole sequence of Marcel and Ross having fun in the city.]
> [“看来我们成功了”开始播放，我们进入了一整段马赛尔和罗斯在城里玩得很开心的场景。]

---

🎬 *The movie set. Monica and Rachel are saying goodbye to Jean-Claude*
> 🎬 *电影布景。莫妮卡和瑞秋正在和让·克劳德道别*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：work out / between you and me / 虚拟语气

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1827.6" duration="7.704" /> **VAN DAMME:** [to Rachel] <Note>I'm sorry it didn't work out between you and me</Note>, [to Monica] or you and me. Drew was very disappointed.
> [对瑞秋]我很抱歉你和我[对莫妮卡]或你和我之间没有结果。德鲁非常失望。
<GrammarTip points="短语动词,介词">

- **I'm sorry (that) it didn't work out**：**work out**"（关系）成了/有结果"；sorry + 宾语从句（台：名词从句）（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）
- **between you and me**：**between + 宾格**——"你我之间"（注意 × between you and I 是常见错误）（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)/[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1835.304" duration="4.128" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** OK, well, bye. [kisses him]
> 好的，再见。[吻他]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1839.432" duration="0.816" /> **VAN DAMME:** Goodbye.
> 再见。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1840.248" duration="4.008" /> **MONICA GELLER:** Well, bye for me too. [kisses him]
> 好吧，我也再见。[吻他]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1844.256" duration="4.824" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** OK, well, bye-bye again. [kisses him again]
> 好吧，再见。[再次吻他]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1849.08" duration="1.608" /> **MONICA GELLER:** OK.
> 好的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1850.688" duration="3.072" /> **VAN DAMME:** Perhaps, uh, <Note>the three of us</Note>, just could. . .
> 也许，呃，我们三个，就可以。
<GrammarTip points="名词词组">

- **the three of us**："我们仨"——the + 数词 + of + 宾格代词（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

MONICA AND **RACHEL GREEN: **Oh, no no no no no.

> 莫妮卡和**瑞秋·格林：**哦，不，不。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1853.76" duration="2.856" /> **VAN DAMME:** Are you sure, <Note>I can crush a walnut with my butt</Note>.
> 你确定吗，我能用我的屁股碾碎核桃。
<GrammarTip points="情态动词,介词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">I</span> <span class="v">can crush</span> <span class="o">a walnut</span> <span class="a">with my butt</span></p>

- **can + 原形 crush**：can 表能力"我能……"；**with + 工具**"用屁股"（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

MONICA AND **RACHEL GREEN: **No no no no.

> 莫妮卡和**瑞秋·格林：**不，不，不。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1856.616" duration="1.752" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Impressive.
> 令人印象深刻。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1858.368" duration="3.792" /> **MONICA GELLER:** But no. <Note>Maybe if I were baking</Note>.
> 但是没有。如果我在烤的话。
<GrammarTip points="虚拟语气">

- **Maybe if I were baking (I could use it)**：**if + were** 与现在事实相反的虚拟——"（等我）哪天要烤点心（倒是用得上）"；主句省略（[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）

</GrammarTip>

MONICA AND **RACHEL GREEN: **Bye-bye.

> 莫妮卡和**瑞秋·格林：**再见。

---

🎬 *City street. Whole gang is there seeing off Marcel.*
> 🎬 *城市街。整个帮派都在那里为马塞尔送行。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：keep + 宾语 + 分词 / 完成进行式 / 双关

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1862.16" duration="9.6" /> **ROSS GELLER:** Bye Marcel. <Note>See ya on the big screen</Note>. <Note>You keep people drinkin' that beer</Note>, OK. I miss you buddy.
> 再见，马塞尔。大屏幕上见。你让人们喝啤酒，好吗。我想你，伙计。
<GrammarTip points="分词,介词">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">You</span> <span class="v">keep</span> <span class="o">people</span> <span class="c">drinkin' that beer</span></p>

- **keep + 宾语 + 现在分词**："让大家一直喝那啤酒"——分词 drinkin' 作**宾语补足语**（台：补语）表持续动作（[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md)/[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）
- **on the big screen**：on 表"在（银幕）上"——"大银幕上见"（[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）

</GrammarTip>

[Marcel is driven off in a limo]
> [马塞尔乘坐豪华轿车离开]

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1871.76" duration="2.856" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** You know, <Note>I think I want to write a song about all this</Note>.
> 你知道，我想我想写一首关于这一切的歌。
<GrammarTip points="名词从句,不定词">

- **I think (that) I want to write a song about all this**：think 后省略 that 的宾语从句；**want + 不定式**（台：不定词）to write（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1874.616" duration="1.536" /> **RACHEL GREEN:** Oh yeah.
> 哦，是的。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1876.152" duration="6.264" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** Yeah. Oh, except <Note>one of the strings on my guitar is broken</Note>. Hey, Chandler, <Note>can I borrow your G-string</Note>?
> 是的。哦，除了我吉他上的一根弦断了。嘿，钱德勒，我能借用一下你的丁字裤吗？
<GrammarTip points="名词词组,双关">

- **one of the strings on my guitar is broken**：**one of + 复数名词**作主语、动词用单数 is；on my guitar 介词短语后置定语（[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **G-string**：双关——既是吉他"G 弦"，也是"丁字裤"（接着调侃 Chandler）（[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1882.416" duration="2.904" /> **CHANDLER BING:** <Note>How long you been waitin' to say that</Note>?
> 你等了多久才这么说？
<GrammarTip points="动词时态,省略">

- **How long (have) you been waitin' to say that?**：口语省略 have 的**现在完成进行式**——"这梗你憋多久了？"；**wait + 不定式** to say（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)/[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md)）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1885.32" duration="3.336" /> **Phoebe Buffay:** About 20 minutes. CLOSING CREDITS
> 大约20分钟。期末学分

---

🎬 *Filming a scene from the movie. Joey is dying on a gurney, Van Damme is looking over him.*
> 🎬 *拍摄电影中的一个场景。乔伊在轮床上奄奄一息，范·达姆正在看着他。*

🎯 **本场景语法重点**：否定疑问句 / what 名词从句 / 进行式与补语对比

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1888.656" duration="3.0" /> **VAN DAMME:** <Note>Can't you see what's going on here</Note>, <Note>this man is dying</Note>.
> 你看不出这里发生了什么，这个人快死了。
<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,疑问句,名词从句">

**原句回听**：<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1888.656" duration="3.0" />

**原句**：Can't you see what's going on here? This man is dying.

**层次划分**：

- **Can't you see...?**：**否定疑问句**——"你难道看不出来……？"（[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md)/[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)）
- <p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">can't see</span> <span class="o">what's going on here</span></p> **what's going on here**：**what 引导宾语从句**（台：名词从句），what 在从句中作主语——"这儿出了什么事"；go on"发生"（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)/[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md)）
- **this man is dying**：**现在进行式**——"这人快不行了"（对比下文 this man is dead）（[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

**翻译**：你看不出这儿怎么回事吗？这个人快死了！

**语法要点**：否定疑问（Ch.22）；what 名词从句（Ch.12）

</GrammarTip>

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1891.656" duration="1.92" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Aaaaagggghhhhh.
> 啊啊啊。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1893.576" duration="0.744" /> **DIRECTOR:** Cut.
> 停。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1894.32" duration="3.0" /> **VAN DAMME:** Can't you see what's going on here, this man is dying.
> 你看不出这里发生了什么，这个人快死了。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1897.32" duration="1.92" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Aaaaagggghhhhh.
> 啊啊啊。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1899.24" duration="0.744" /> **DIRECTOR:** Cut.
> 停。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1899.984" duration="3.0" /> **VAN DAMME:** Can't you see what's going on here, this man is dying.
> 你看不出这里发生了什么，这个人快死了。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1902.984" duration="3.384" /> **JOEY TRIBBIANI:** Aaaaagggghhhhh. Mommy
> 啊啊啊。妈妈

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1906.368" duration="0.72" /> **DIRECTOR:** Cut.
> 停。

<AudioButton src="/audio/episodes/S02/S02E12.mp3" start="1907.088" duration="2.736" /> **VAN DAMME:** Can't you see what's going on here, <Note>this man is dead</Note>.
> 你没看到这里发生了什么吗，这个人死了。
<GrammarTip points="补语,对比">

<p class="svoc"><span class="s">this man</span> <span class="v">is</span> <span class="c">dead</span></p>

- **is dead vs is dying**：**dead 是形容词作主语补语**——"已经死了"（状态）；**is dying** 是进行式——"正在死"（过程）。Joey 一路 NG，Van Damme 干脆改台词（[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md)/[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md)）

</GrammarTip>

END
> 结束

---

📚 **术语对照**（大陆教材 / 台湾《语法俱乐部》）：定语从句 = 关系从句；宾语从句/主语从句/表语从句 = 名词从句；状语从句 = 副词从句；介词 = 介系词；系动词 = 连缀动词；宾语补足语 = 补语；不定式 = 不定词；主谓一致 = 主语动词一致性

---

<GrammarTip type="pattern" points="句型总复习,使役动词与宾语补语">

**本集 S+V+O+C 句型总复习**（[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md)）：

- **make + O + 形容词**：That commercial always makes me so sad.
<p class="svoc"><span class="s">That commercial</span> <span class="v">makes</span> <span class="o">me</span> <span class="c">so sad</span></p>
- **let + O + 原形**：let me see those hands / He's gonna let me have him / Would you let me talk
<p class="svoc"><span class="s">you</span> <span class="v">'d let</span> <span class="o">me</span> <span class="c">talk</span></p>
- **keep/have + O + 分词**：You keep people drinkin' that beer（现在分词）/ have your shirt tucked into them（过去分词）
<p class="svoc"><span class="s">You</span> <span class="v">keep</span> <span class="o">people</span> <span class="c">drinkin'</span></p>
- **感官动词 see + O + 分词**：I saw you today kissing in the doctor's lounge.
- **注意**：make/let/see 后接**原形**；want/ask/get/tell 后接 **to + 原形**（want me to wear / asked me to wear / get me to bribe）——两类动词补语形式不同，别混！

</GrammarTip>

<GrammarTip type="pattern" points="句型总复习,名词从句">

**本集名词从句（台：名词从句）四种连词**（[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md)）：

- **that（可省）**：know that I love you / I feel that this is totally unjustified / told me I was the only one
- **if/whether（是否）**：I wonder if I did the right thing / wants to know if the monkey's ready / tell me if you're still upset
- **what（= the thing that）**：guess what I got / It's not what you think / that's what I mean / what's going on here
- **how/where/why/when**：That's how radio stars escape stalkers / where we hung out with Drew Barrymore / remember why this started / know when vegetables are done
- **口诀**：从句内一律**陈述语序**（× where is he → where he is）；think 的否定要**前移**（I don't think we should...）

</GrammarTip>

<GrammarTip type="pattern" points="句型总复习,委婉与虚拟">

**本集"委婉/虚拟"语气大全**（[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md)/[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md)）：

- **过去进行式表委婉**：I was thinking it would be kind of sexy if... / I was just thinking / they were kinda hopin' that you'd play...
- **if + 过去式（与现在相反）**：if you wore mine tonight / if we were in prison, you guys would be my bitches / If you didn't have your shirt tucked in
- **would 表假设**：that would be fantastic / my watch would fall off / what would you be wearing?
- **should have + 过去分词（事后诸葛）**：someone should have called me / I should have told you this a long time ago
- **don't have to（不必）≠ must not（不准）**：you don't have to tell me anything

</GrammarTip>

<GrammarTip type="long" points="长难句,本集语法地图">

**S02E12 全集语法地图**——把本集知识点放回《语法俱乐部》坐标系：

- **[Ch.1](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter01.md) 基本句型**：makes me so sad（S+V+O+C）/ she bought you a train（S+V+O+O）/ you left the toilet seat up（S+V+O+C）/ There's an old saying（There be）/ this man is dead（S+V+C）
- **[Ch.2](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter02.md) 名词词组**：my very own stalker / a friend of his（双重所有格）/ one of her eyebrows / the three of us / a ton of cool responsibilities / Harry Elefante（同位语）
- **[Ch.3](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter03.md) 时态**：has passed on（完成式）/ we've never met / haven't seen my monkey in almost a year / is gonna be making out with Gabe Kaplan（未来进行）/ somebody's been doing（完成进行）/ are you on your way over（进行式表将来）
- **[Ch.4](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter04.md) 不定式**：seems to work out / refuse to acknowledge / be supposed to come here / I'll get to see your underwear / too chicken to tell / be dying to have a threesome / what to say（疑问词+不定式）
- **[Ch.5](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter05.md) 动名词**：enjoy being with other men / be used to playing for grown-ups / end up drinking it yourself / miss hearing about the specials / quit flicking / stop seeing him / keep talking about you
- **[Ch.6](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter06.md) 分词**：Your monkey was taken（被动）/ the last guy got fired / got caught masturbating / saw you kissing（宾补）/ have your shirt tucked / kids laughing（独立结构）
- **[Ch.7](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter07.md) 形容词比较**：the best doctor in all of Salem（最高级）/ it's a lot cuter / how big of a star / what would be even sexier / the most ridiculous / much better than me
- **[Ch.8](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter08.md) 副词**：not...anymore / just + 过去式（Did you just flick me?）/ all of the sudden / ever + 完成式 / so much thinner / kind of
- **[Ch.9](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter09.md) 语气**：someone should have called me / Normally, I would not do it / I could just eat them / don't have to ≠ must not / Don't you dare / How come 不倒装
- **[Ch.10](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter10.md) 介词·短语动词**：remind me of Marcel / answers to the name / took off with my clothes / walks off with / blowin' me off / hook up with / making up for it / sold me out / get heat from
- **[Ch.11](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter11.md) 主谓一致**：nobody ever tells / Does anybody need anything? / No one was around / one of the virus victims called in sick / one of the strings is broken
- **[Ch.12](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter12.md) 名词从句**：wonder if I did the right thing / guess what I got / the fact that they're both monkeys（同位语从句）/ that's how we get hamburgers / I don't think we should see each other（否定前移）/ Do you have any idea how high up this thing goes
- **[Ch.13](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter13.md) 副词从句**：'till he realizes he's a monkey（现在式代未来）/ when we pass her on the stairs / just in case she isn't a cartoon / just because...doesn't mean / unless someone convinces her / if we were in prison（虚拟）
- **[Ch.14](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter14.md) 关系从句**：the monkey I donated last year（作宾语可省）/ the one who wants to boff the maniac / a world where Joey is a neuro-surgeon / the worst that could happen / all (that) I know / everything the zoo tells ya
- **[Ch.15](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter15.md) 对等连接词**：祈使句 + or（否则）/ either but or butter / and asking, and asking（排比）/ he got sick, and then...but then（叙事排比）
- **[Ch.16](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter16.md) 简化从句**：flitting out of his cave like a winged messenger（分词短语）/ kids laughing（独立结构）/ Enclosed please find（书信简化）
- **[Ch.22](../../语法俱乐部/Chapter22.md) 倒装**：there goes my whole belief system（全部倒装）/ there he is（代词不倒装）/ How come I'm walking with you（不倒装）/ Who am I to question...? / Can't you see...?

建议：先通读剧集→按此地图回到《语法俱乐部》对应章节精读→再回剧本找例句巩固。

</GrammarTip>
